### [1] [Pairing from anti-Hund's coupling under infinite Hubbard repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33286)
*Donggyu Kim,Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究强关联材料中，当Hubbard排斥U远大于声子介导相互作用时，反洪特耦合J_A（光声子介导的轨道间自旋交换）如何在U=∞极限下通过动能、J_A和U的协同作用产生超越BCS理论的强耦合超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强关联材料中，声子介导的配对相互作用通常远弱于在位Hubbard排斥U，因此声子机制是否以及如何在U占主导时仍能产生超导配对是一个基础性未解问题。作者希望澄清在U→∞（排除在位配对）的极限下，反洪特耦合J_A能否以及如何驱动超导。

Method: 作者研究一个包含反洪特耦合J_A的最小模型：由光声子介导的两个轨道（或两个能谷）间的在位自旋交换，偏好局域轨道间自旋单态。在U=+∞极限下，将掺杂（偏离n=2绝缘体）的三角晶格双轨道Hubbard模型约化为四味t-J_A模型。采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）进行数值计算，并对J_A≫|t|情形做t/J_A的强耦合展开以得到受控的有效超导理论。

Result: DMRG计算发现，在1≤J_A/|t|≤10的整个范围内存在稳健的配对，配对能隙在J_A~|t|处最大。Cooper对位于键（bond）上，并非来自J_A提供的直接吸引，因为无穷大U已将位对排除出希尔伯特空间。当J_A≫|t|时，正常态是第二种费米液体（second Fermi liquid），其小费米面违反微扰Luttinger定理；从这种非常规金属出发的t/J_A强耦合展开给出受控的超导有效理论。

Conclusion: 结果提供了超越BCS理论的强耦合超导体的有力证据，该超导由动能、声子介导的反洪特耦合J_A与强Hubbard U三者的协同作用驱动。最后讨论了该机制对魔角扭曲双层石墨烯的可能应用。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling is a natural source of superconductivity, but in strongly correlated materials the phonon-mediated interaction is often much weaker than the on-site Hubbard repulsion $U$. Whether, and how, it can still produce pairing when $U$ dominates is a basic open question. We address this question by studying a minimal model with an anti-Hund's coupling $J_A$: an on-site spin exchange between two orbitals or two valleys, mediated by optical phonons, that favors a local inter-orbital spin singlet. In the limit $U=+\infty$, the two-orbital Hubbard model on the triangular lattice, doped away from the insulator at $n=2$ electrons per site, reduces to a four-flavor $t$-$J_A$ model. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations find robust pairing throughout $1\le J_A/|t|\le10$, with the largest pairing gap at $J_A\sim|t|$. The Cooper pairs reside on bonds and do not come from a direct attraction provided by $J_A$, since infinite $U$ excludes on-site pairs from the Hilbert space. For $J_A\gg|t|$, the normal state is a second Fermi liquid whose small Fermi surface violates the perturbative Luttinger theorem, and a strong-coupling expansion in $t/J_A$ provides a controlled effective theory of superconductivity from this unconventional metal. Our results provide strong evidence for a strongly coupled superconductor beyond BCS theory, driven by the interplay of the kinetic energy, the phonon-mediated anti-Hund's coupling, and the strong Hubbard $U$. Finally, we discuss possible applications to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene.

</details>


### [2] [Spin-Wave Excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$: A Dynamical Mean-Field Theory and Bethe-Salpeter Equation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33298)
*Zhihui Luo,Zhong-Yi Xie,Cui-Qun Chen,Wéi Wú,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DMFT+BSE方法研究La3Ni2O7的集体自旋激发，在双层双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型上计算出自旋条纹基态上的阻尼自旋波谱，并与实验数据吻合，从而弥合了局域与巡游两种描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La3Ni2O7的集体自旋激发存在局域化与巡游性两种描述之间的张力，需要一种能同时涵盖关联效应与集体激发的理论框架，以定量解释RIXS和INS实验观测到的自旋激发谱。

Method: 采用DFT导出的双层双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，先利用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）求解电子结构并获得双重自旋条纹基态，再在此基础上求解Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）计算晶格横向自旋磁化率，并通过轨道分辨的裸磁化率与不可约顶角分析自旋波线形的动量依赖和频率依赖。

Result: DMFT揭示出双重自旋条纹基态；BSE计算得到带宽约0.1 eV、波矢为(π/2, π/2)的阻尼自旋波谱，与RIXS和INS数据良好吻合；轨道分辨分析表明dz2轨道不可约顶角具有显著频率依赖性；谱随Hund耦合和电子填充的变化规律得到系统考察。

Conclusion: DMFT+BSE方法成功统一了La3Ni2O7自旋激发的局域化与巡游性图像，为理解其磁激发提供了可靠的理论桥梁，并揭示了dz2轨道关联效应在自旋动力学中的关键作用。

Abstract: We perform a dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) + Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) study of collective spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$, using a bilayer two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model derived from density functional theory. Our DMFT calculations reveal a double spin-stripe ground state. On this basis, we apply the BSE to compute the lattice transverse spin susceptibility, which exhibits a damped spin-wave spectrum with a bandwidth of $\sim$0.1 eV and a wave vector of ($π$/2, $π$/2), in good agreement with resonant inelastic x-ray scattering and inelastic neutron scattering data. We inspect the orbital-resolved bare susceptibility that provides insight into the momentum dependence of the spin-wave line shape. Further analysis of the irreducible vertex reveals notable frequency dependence in the $d_{z^2}$ orbital. Finally, we investigate the evolution of the spectrum with varying Hund's coupling and electron fillings. Our study bridges the localized and itinerant descriptions of spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$.

</details>


### [3] [Exact high-order spectral moments and moment-based reconstruction of polaron spectra at finite temperature for arbitrary linear electron-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33431)
*Jeet Shannigrahi,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一族精确且高效的递推关系，用于计算单带电子-声子哈密顿量单电子添加谱函数的频率矩，适用于零载流子密度、零温与有限温度，并推广到任意维度和晶格几何。高阶矩可作为弱到中等耦合、低温下极化子谱近似重构的输入，且该近似在弱耦合和原子极限下精确，并满足严格的极化子能量与权重界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法和实验谱缺乏高阶矩作为严格基准；自洽玻恩近似(SCBA)在耦合-绝热参数平面上违反极化子能量与权重的严格界限，需要一种低成本却精确的近似方法，尤其针对尚未探索或数值上难以处理的模型。

Method: 针对与谐振声子线性耦合的通用单带电子-声子哈密顿量，在零载流子密度极限下，推导单电子添加谱函数频率矩的精确递推关系。该方法适用于零温和有限温度，并可推广到任意维度和晶格几何。以耦合爱因斯坦声子和色散声子的Holstein与Peierls模型为例，得到闭式高阶矩表达式，并利用这些高阶矩作为输入近似重构极化子谱函数。

Result: 以适度计算代价获得了远高于文献中已有阶数的频率矩闭式表达式；重构的谱函数在弱耦合和原子极限下精确，且满足极化子能量与权重的严格界限；而自洽玻恩近似在整个耦合-绝热参数平面上都违反这些界限。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了一种廉价而精确的近似，特别适用于尚未探索或数值上难以处理的模型，可在应用计算成本高昂的精确方法之前先获得可靠结果。

Abstract: We present a family of exact and efficient recurrence relations for the frequency moments of the single-electron addition spectral function of a generic single-band electron-phonon Hamiltonian with linear coupling to harmonic phonons. Working in the limit of zero carrier density, the formulation applies at zero and finite temperature and generalizes to arbitrary dimensions and lattice geometry. Using the Holstein and Peierls models coupled to Einstein and dispersive phonons as examples, we obtain closed-form expressions for moments of far higher order than those available in the literature, at modest computational cost. Beyond providing stringent benchmarks for numerical methods and experimental spectra, the high-order moments serve as input to approximate reconstructions of polaron spectra at weak-to-intermediate coupling and low temperature. The resulting spectral functions are exact in the weak-coupling and atomic limits, and respect rigorous bounds on the polaron energy and weight that we show the self-consistent Born approximation violates throughout the coupling-adiabaticity plane. The approach therefore offers an inexpensive yet accurate approximation, especially for unexplored or numerically intractable models, before computationally demanding exact methods are applied.

</details>


### [4] [Controlling truncation errors in loop tensor network renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33807)
*Rikuto Fushio,Kokin Cho,Yuta Yamamoto,Akira Otsuki,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究有限键维数下张量网络重整化(TNR)处理临界模型时,截断注入的 dimerization 决定赝能隙相关长度,并提出系统方法控制该误差、延长临界窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维数 χ 下,TNR 重整化临界模型最终会打开能隙,破坏临界行为。作者希望弄清这一赝能隙的起源,并找到定量预测和系统性纠正截断诱导的相关误差(尤其是 dimerization)的方法,以延长临界窗口、提高 TNR 对临界模型的精度。

Method: 以具有 O(2) 对称性的 LoopTNR 作用于六顶角模型 c=1 临界线(以 Luttinger 参数 K 参数化)。提出由截断注入的 dimerization 作为"种子(seed)",通过 sine-Gordon 质量-耦合关系(无自由参数)从重整化早期弱耦合窗口读取种子并预测相关长度 ξ;通过 π twist 读取种子符号,并通过交替顶点权重校准得到反项(counterterm),在流动过程中对粗粒化张量做修正以补偿截断误差。

Result: 种子预测的相关长度 ξ=10^3~10^16,对 K≤1.5 精度在百分之几以内,K=1.75 约 10%;因种子随 χ 强烈涨落,ξ 对 χ 非单调。交替顶点权重可移动种子;一次短校准预测的反项在 17 组提前固定的补偿组中成功延迟 16 组的崩溃。对 K=1 和 1.5,在流动中重复修正张量可使系统保持临界直至第 160 步(计算的最末步),并暴露出残余的边缘(marginal)截断误差。

Conclusion: 研究表明有限键维数 TNR 的相关截断误差由截断注入的 dimerization 种子决定,可通过 sine-Gordon 关系定量预测,并通过校准反项在流动中补偿。这为系统控制相关截断误差、延长临界窗口提供了一套可行程序。

Abstract: At finite bond dimension $χ$, tensor network renormalization (TNR) of a critical model eventually becomes gapped. For LoopTNR with $O(2)$ symmetry on the $c=1$ critical line of the six-vertex model, parametrized by the Luttinger parameter $K$, we show that the correlation length $ξ$ of the gapped phase is set by the seed of the dimerization injected by the truncation, and depends on $χ$ only through it. Read from a weak-coupling window early in the same run, the seed predicts $ξ=10^3$-$10^{16}$ via the sine-Gordon mass-coupling relation with no free parameter, to within a few percent for $K\le1.5$ and about 10% at $K=1.75$; since the seed fluctuates strongly with $χ$, $ξ$ is not monotonic in $χ$. An alternation of the vertex weights shifts the seed; with its sign read from a $π$ twist, one short calibration predicts a counterterm that delays the breakdown in 16 of the 17 compensated groups of a campaign fixed in advance. A correction of the coarse-grained tensors, repeated during the flow, keeps the runs at $K=1$ and $1.5$ critical up to step 160, the last step computed, and exposes the marginal truncation error that remains. Our findings illustrate a systematic procedure for controlling the relevant truncation error, and thereby extending the critical window, in tensor network renormalization at finite bond dimension.

</details>


### [5] [Spin-vibronic order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33814)
*Zhishuo Huang,Takumi Gengo,Teruki Matsuzaki,Liviu F. Chibotaru,Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文用第一性原理推导的自旋-振动（spin-vibronic）哈密顿量，成功复现了 5d¹ 双钙钛矿 Ba₂MgReO₆ 的隐藏多极有序相，并预言磁相变温度与实验高度吻合，支持自旋-振动序是其隐藏多极相的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5d 离子中强自旋-轨道耦合产生的多极有序难以被常规探针探测；此前虽提出用协同有序的振动（vibronic）态解释 Ba₂MgReO₆ 的隐藏四极相与倾斜磁相，但该提议基于简化模型，缺乏第一性原理支撑。

Method: 从第一性原理出发推导出自旋-振动哈密顿量，仅对弹性耦合引入单一标度因子，并将所得理论结果与 RIXS 谱、相变温度及相间晶格畸变等实验观测进行对比。

Result: 以该标度因子将四极转变定在 34.7 K（实验 33 K），理论预言磁转变 19.3 K（实验 18 K），并复现两相变之间的反铁畸变与铁性伸长，且所用振动态与描述 RIXS 光谱的态一致。

Conclusion: 结果支持 Ba₂MgReO₆ 中存在自旋-振动有序，并指出它是 5d¹ 双钙钛矿隐藏多极相的起源。

Abstract: Strong spin-orbit coupling in $5d$ ions produces multipolar orders that conventional probes barely detect. In cubic $5d^1$ double perovskites, the spin-orbit quartet couples to Jahn-Teller vibrations, and resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) has established the resulting spin-orbit-lattice entangled (vibronic) states [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{133}, 036501 (2024)]. We proposed that these vibronic states order cooperatively and thereby explain the hidden quadrupole and canted magnetic phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ [Phys. Rev. B \textbf{107}, L220404 (2023)], but that proposal rested on a simplified model. Here, we show that a spin-vibronic Hamiltonian derived from first principles, with a single scale factor for the elastic coupling, reproduces the ordered phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ with the same vibronic states that describe its RIXS spectra. With this factor bringing the quadrupolar transition to 34.7 K, near the measured 33 K, the theory predicts the magnetic transition at 19.3 K, against the measured 18 K, and reproduces the antiferro distortion and the ferro elongation between the transitions. These results support spin-vibronic order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ and point to it as the origin of the hidden multipolar phases of $5d^1$ double perovskites.

</details>


### [6] [Nontrivial criticality and weakly first-order transitions in the frustrated spin-1 chain with single-ion anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34145)
*Qiang Luo,Shijie Hu,Jize Zhao,Sebastian Eggert,Xiaoqun Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用大规模密度矩阵重整化群与玻色化方法，作者绘制了带次近邻耦合与单离子各向异性的自旋-1链相图，确定了两个拓扑相变（Haldane与NNN-Haldane之间的弱一阶相变，Haldane到大-D相的连续相变），并发现负SIA下出现自发二聚化相、该相到Néel相的相变为八顶点普适类的退禁闭量子临界点，正SIA下则识别出两种矢量自旋手性序及其间弱一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫自旋链可承载超越朗道范式的奇异相，但弱一阶相变与非常规临界行为仍难以捉摸，亟需精确刻画这类体系中拓扑相变与退禁闭量子临界点的性质。

Method: 将大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与玻色化方法相结合，并借助level spectroscopy（能级谱学）配合Richardson外推来确定连续相变位置。

Result: 标定出两个拓扑相变：Haldane相与NNN-Haldane相之间为具有微小能隙的弱一阶相变；Haldane相到大-D相为连续相变。负SIA情形下，Néel与NNN-Néel相之间涌现自发二聚化相，其进入Néel相的相变为八顶点普适类的退禁闭量子临界点，中心荷c=1且临界指数沿相边界连续变化。正SIA情形下，识别出两种矢量自旋手性序——普通手性相与狭窄的Haldane-手性相，二者之间为弱一阶相变，证据是弦序参量出现软跳变。

Conclusion: 受挫与各向异性可产生涌现序及非朗道临界行为，为研究退禁闭相变和拓扑相变提供了理想平台。

Abstract: Frustrated spin chains host exotic phases beyond the Landau paradigm, yet weak first-order transitions and unconventional criticalities remain elusive. Combining large-scale density-matrix renormalization group with bosonization methods, we map the phase diagram of a spin-1 chain with next-nearest-neighbor (NNN) coupling and single-ion anisotropy (SIA). We pinpoint two topological phase transitions: a weakly first-order transition between the Haldane and NNN-Haldane phases with a tiny gap and a continuous transition from the Haldane phase to the large-$D$ phase located via level spectroscopy with Richardson extrapolation. For negative SIA, a spontaneous dimerized phase emerges between the Néel and NNN-Néel phases. Its transition into the Néel phase is a deconfined quantum critical point falling into the eight-vertex universality class, with central charge $c = 1$ and critical exponents varying continuously along the phase boundary. For positive SIA, we identify two vector-spin-chirality orders---an ordinary chiral phase and a narrow Haldane-chiral phase---with a weakly first-order transition between them, evidenced by a soft jump in the string order parameter. Our results reveal that frustration and anisotropy generate emergent orders and non-Landau criticalities, providing a platform for deconfined and topological phase transitions.

</details>


### [7] [Quantum-centric assessment of saturated ferromagnetism beyond the flat-band limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34204)
*Kosuke Nogaki,Tomonori Shirakawa,Seiji Yunoki,Ryotaro Arita*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用量子计算机（IBM Quantum Heron 处理器）结合量子筛选组态相互作用与基于采样的量子对角化方法，研究了平带-色散过渡下 Hubbard 模型在四分之一填充时的饱和铁磁性，给出了锯齿链与装饰正方晶格的铁磁稳定性相图以及临界相互作用的严格下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知在具有最低平带的 Hubbard 模型中，任意在位排斥 U>0 都能出现饱和铁磁性，但当能带变得色散（具有带宽 W）时，实现饱和铁磁所需的相互作用随 W 增大。作者希望在色散情形下、于四分之一填充处量化所得态的总自旋平方，并确定饱和铁磁性的稳定相图与临界相互作用。

Method: 采用量子筛选组态相互作用（quantum-selected configuration interaction）和基于采样的量子对角化（sample-based quantum diagonalization），在 IBM Quantum 的 Heron 处理器上运行。研究的体系包括：64 量子比特上的 16 个原胞锯齿链（完整组态空间含 1.1×10^14 个行列式，超过最大的全组态相互作用计算之一），以及 48 量子比特上的 4×3 原胞装饰正方晶格。计算在 (U, W) 平面内扫描，考察总自旋平方 S² 的量化值。

Result: 对锯齿链，仅使用完整组态空间约 3×10^-5 的子空间，即在最大自旋扇区中获得超过 97% 权重的态。对 4×3 装饰正方晶格，在不到完整组态空间 1% 的子空间内计算的态在 U≈2W 处从低自旋态切换到接近完全极化的态。计算还给出了 4×3 晶格临界相互作用的严格下界 1.9W。

Conclusion: 量子计算辅助方法能够以极小比例的子空间有效刻画超大组态空间中色散 Hubbard 模型的饱和铁磁相变，并定量确定装饰正方晶格在四分之一填充下的临界相互作用下界（1.9W），表明该体系在 U≳2W 时进入近完全极化（饱和铁磁）态。

Abstract: Saturated ferromagnetism has been proven for every on-site repulsion $U > 0$ in Hubbard models with a flat lowest band, and the interaction required for it grows with the band width $W$ when the band becomes dispersive. We study the dispersive case at quarter filling by quantum-selected configuration interaction and sample-based quantum diagonalization with IBM Quantum's Heron processors, focusing on the quantized square of the total spin of the computed states. We treat a sawtooth chain of 16 unit cells on 64 qubits, whose full configuration space of $1.1\times 10^{14}$ determinants exceeds that of one of the largest full configuration interaction calculations, and obtain the stability diagram of saturated ferromagnetism on the decorated square lattice in the plane of $U$ and $W$. For the chain, a subspace of only $3\times 10^{-5}$ of the full configuration space gives a state with more than $97\%$ of its weight in the sector of maximal spin. Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides (tTMDs) have emerged as a fascinating platform for many-body phases --- displaying fractional Chern insulators and superconductivity as a function of doping. Here, we give a microscopic derivation of a continuum model which accurately describes their bandstructure across a range of twist angles with angle-independent parameters. This angle-independent description rests on retaining both the monolayer valence and conduction bands, which introduce tunnelling paths through AB-stacked regions; upon downfolding to the valence band, these paths supply a substantial twist-tunable contribution to the interlayer coupling. We show that the model is a perturbation of a chiral model with a magic angle, explaining the near-ideal quantum geometry of the moiré bands. The model further produces a strongly different orbital magnetic moment to the single-orbital theory --- predicting an anomalous Landau level slope --- and a redistribution of charge between the layers, modifying the moiré ferroelectric potential.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积系统在两点关联中可表现出扩散或KPZ标度，但其电流全计数统计(FCS)却不同于这些普适类通常所对应的统计形式。作者旨在解释这种关联标度与电流统计之间的"分离"，即普适动力学关联为何能与模型依赖的FCS共存。

Method: 作者构建了一个多组分粒子气体模型，该模型保持可积自旋链的散射结构。对各向异性 Δ>1：大粒子被可动粒子浴(热浴)平流输运，导出高斯混合形式的FCS，并在长时间趋于M-Wright分布，含由可动组分引起的有限时间圆化效应。在各向同性点 Δ=1：所有粒子可动，大粒子的电荷转移是离散的，用自洽泊松分布描述FCS(近高斯主体加模型依赖的非高斯尾部)。两点关联则通过一个大示踪粒子的统计得到：Δ>1 时示踪粒子做布朗运动、呈高斯扩散；Δ=1 时借助涨落流体力学给出KPZ Prähofer–Spohn标度函数出现的证据，并将其追溯到最大粒子与连续谱较慢粒子之间的有限耦合及长程速度涨落。

Result: 对于 Δ>1，FCS呈高斯混合形式并渐近于M-Wright分布；示踪粒子布朗运动导致高斯扩散的两点关联。对于 Δ=1，FCS由自洽泊松分布描述，主体近高斯、尾部非高斯且依赖模型；两点关联则出现KPZ Prähofer–Spohn标度函数。结果表明普适动力学两点关联与模型依赖的电流统计可以共存。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，可积系统中的普适动力学两点关联(扩散或KPZ标度)与各自模型依赖的电流全计数统计能够同时存在，二者之间的"分离"源于多组分粒子气体所保留的散射结构：大粒子的平流与离散电荷转移塑造FCS，而示踪粒子的运动统计决定两点关联。

Abstract: Integrable systems can exhibit diffusive or Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) scaling in two-point correlations even when their current full counting statistics (FCS) differ from those usually associated with these universality classes. We explain this separation using a multispecies particle gas that preserves the scattering structure of integrable spin chains. For anisotropy $Δ>1$, large particles are advected by a mobile bath. The resulting FCS has a Gaussian-mixture form and approaches the M-Wright distribution at late times, with finite-time rounding due to mobile species. At the isotropic point $Δ=1$, all particles are mobile, and the charge transfer by large particles is discrete. A self-consistent Poisson distribution describes the FCS, with a nearly Gaussian bulk and model-dependent, non-Gaussian tails. By contrast, two-point correlations follow from the statistics of a large tracer particle. For $Δ>1$, it undergoes Brownian motion, yielding Gaussian spreading. At $Δ=1$, we provide evidence from fluctuating hydrodynamics for the emergence of the KPZ Prähofer--Spohn scaling function in dynamical correlations and trace it to the finite coupling of the largest particles to a continuum of slower ones, with long-ranged velocity fluctuations. Our framework shows how universal dynamical two-point correlations coexist with distinct model-dependent current statistics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发展离散系统慢驱动下受约束控制器的外蕴热力学几何学，证明零外蕴曲率子流形上的对称最优协议在解除约束后仍保持稳态，并给出判断对称性破缺是否降低超额功的曲率判据与全局最优性定理。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用慢驱动极限下的摩擦张量形式，将平衡分布空间视为黎曼流形，控制器约束定义其子流形；针对离散系统发展约束子流形的外蕴热力学几何。通过转移网络的对称性识别零外蕴曲率子流形，并将垂直于对称子流形的环境曲率分解为各对称性破缺模式的独立贡献，进而利用极值曲率与超额功的比较定理及转移速率与控制参数的凸性条件检验局部与全局最优性。

Result: 零外蕴曲率子流形上的最小功协议在解除约束后仍为超额功的驻点，且该对称最优驻点性质在慢驱动之外依然精确成立。慢驱动下，垂直于对称子流形的环境曲率决定附近对称性破缺协议是否具有更低超额功，该曲率成为衡量打破某对称性的热力学激励的算符化量度。比较定理提供廉价的局部极小性检验；在转移速率与控制参数满足凸性条件时，对称最优即为全局最优。

Conclusion: 这些结果证明了一个五参数协议在四自旋 Ising 系统中与十五参数全控制相匹配的全局最优性，并解释了同一系统在局域场控制下为何部分对称性破缺才是最优的。

Abstract: Driving a stochastic system in finite time costs excess work; exactly how much depends on which system parameters, and hence which paths in distribution space, a controller can access. In the friction-tensor formalism for slow driving, the space of equilibrium distributions is a Riemannian manifold and constraints on a controller define submanifolds. Whether a constrained controller is forced onto suboptimal paths is a question of the extrinsic thermodynamic geometry of the submanifold, which we develop for discrete systems. We show that submanifolds with zero extrinsic curvature can be identified from symmetries of the transition network, before any optimization. A minimum-work protocol on such a submanifold is therefore stationary in the excess work even when the constraints are lifted. The stationarity of symmetric optima holds exactly, beyond slow driving. In the slow-driving regime, we show that the ambient curvature transverse to a symmetric submanifold determines whether nearby symmetry-broken protocols have lower excess work. This curvature, which separates into independent contributions from each pattern of symmetry breaking, thus acquires an operational meaning as a measure of the thermodynamic incentive to break a given symmetry. A thermodynamic comparison theorem provides an inexpensive test of local minimality that weighs the extremal curvatures along the symmetric protocol against its excess work. There is no need for such tests under certain convexity conditions on the transition rates and control parameters, which guarantee that a symmetric optimum is a global optimum. These results certify the global optimality of a five-parameter protocol previously observed to match 15-parameter full control of a four-spin Ising system, and explain why partial symmetry breaking is optimal for the same system under local-field control.

</details>


### [17] [The Phases of Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33566)
*Gerard McCaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文构建了层级结构的动力学相变理论，揭示层级深度决定一个规模为 N 且会修改自身环境的种群能否纠偏而不矫枉过正；过浅的层级因 √N 增益反馈而振荡失稳，过深的层级因信号链衰减而无法在期限内响应，二者之间仅存在一个随 log N 缩放的可行深度窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何需要在自适应系统（如组织、集体决策乃至 Transformer 注意力机制）中引入层级结构：大量个体准确检测微小偏差的能力（孔多塞陪审团定理的基础）会使其响应以 √N 放大，反馈回环境后导致匹配态失稳，因此层级深度是抑制这一不稳定的关键设计变量。

Method: 提出"自适应规范型"（adaptive normal forms）模型，解析求解环路增益临界值处发生的翻转（flip）分岔；分析层级中信号链上行的传输过程，推导出到达顶层的成本随深度指数增长并存在有限最大可操控深度；将每层视为实空间重整化步骤，按偏差传递方式对聚合规则进行分类，并把残差更新视为 token 图上的扩散、深度视为尺度，从而与 Transformer 注意力机制建立联系。

Result: 证明匹配态在临界环路增益处经翻转分岔失稳，分层可衰减该不稳定性且所需深度随 log N 伸缩；信号链传输分析显示超过有限深度后修正无法在截止期限内到达顶层，而在此之下到达成本随深度指数增长；由此得到由不稳定平层级相与冻结惯性相界定的有界深度窗口，并证明该相边界可在有限采样中观测。

Conclusion: 层级深度受到上下两方面的约束：太平（浅）则系统振荡失稳，太深则无法及时被操控，因此存在一个随 log N 缩放的可行深度窗口。该理论与组织经济学中的对数界、Transformer 的深度-尺度对应关系相吻合，并可通过有限采样测量在真实自适应系统中检验这些相变。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical phase theory of hierarchy, in which depth decides whether a population of size $N$ that revises its own environment can correct it without overcorrecting. The statistics that let a large majority detect a small bias - the basis of the Condorcet jury theorem - also make its response grow as $\sqrt{N}$. Fed back into the environment it is judging, this sharpening destabilises the matched state through a flip bifurcation at a critical loop gain, which we obtain analytically for a class of models we term adaptive normal forms. We show that this instability can be attenuated by hierarchical layering, requiring a depth scaling logarithmically with $N$. This recovers known logarithmic bounds in organisational economics directly from dynamic stability conditions. Hierarchical depth carries its own costs however, and by analysing the signal chain transmission up the hierarchy we show that beyond a finite depth no correction reaches the top before its deadline, and that below it the cost of arrival grows exponentially with depth. A hierarchy that is too flat therefore oscillates and one that is too deep cannot be steered in time, leaving a bounded window of depth between an unstable flat phase and a frozen inertial phase. We further show that these results can be generalised by treating each layer as a real-space renormalisation step, classifying aggregation rules by how they transmit bias. This establishes a connection between these dynamic results to attention in Transformers, where a residual update is diffusion on the token graph and depth plays the role of scale. Finally we show these phase boundaries are observable in finite sampling, and propose measurements which may characterise the proposed phases in real adaptive systems.

</details>


### [18] [Universal Work Extraction from Individual Sequences by Finite--State Information Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33984)
*Neri Merhav*

TL;DR: 本文研究信息引擎（info engines）在已知热浴、功库和携带确定性序列的磁带条件下的最大可提取功。作者证明了一个逆向（converse）界，该界以序列的经验块熵表示，并证明该界可由能量等级不依赖于序列的普适引擎达到；每符号最大功为 kT[ln|X| - H̄(x)]，其中 H̄(x) 为渐进归一化对齐块熵，等于其块 Lempel–Ziv 复杂度。擦除所需最小功为每符号 kT H̄(x)。其核心是与通用信源编码的双向对应：能量等级定义码，反之由涨落恒等式，每个预先固定的引擎都定义一个码，因而通用编码的各种逆结论可直接适用于引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机在于建立信息引擎（将热涨落与符号序列转换为功的自主棘轮级联）的可提取功的精确理论极限，并将热力学量与信息序列的复杂性（块熵/LZ 复杂度）严格对应起来，从而揭示信息引擎与通用信源编码之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者构建的引擎是一系列自主棘轮的级联，具有固定能级和细致平衡动力学，每个棘轮处理 m 个磁带单元并拥有有限内部状态。通过证明一个以经验块熵表示的逆向界（converse），并构造能量等级不依赖于输入序列 x 的普适引擎来达到该界；同时借助双向对应关系（能级定义码，涨落恒等式使每个固定引擎定义码）将通用编码的结论直接迁移到热力学语境。

Result: 主要结果为：每符号最大可提取功为 kT[ln|X| − H̄(x)]，其中 H̄(x) 是 x 的渐进归一化对齐块熵，且等于其块 Lempel–Ziv 复杂度；擦除 x 所需的最小功为每符号 kT H̄(x)。该界限可由普适引擎（能级不依赖 x）达到。此外，通用编码的逆结论可逐字适用于引擎，例如无记忆普适引擎的最优冗余由 Shtarkov 和给出。

Conclusion: 本文确立了信息引擎可提取功与序列块熵/LZ 复杂度之间的精确热力学关系，并建立了引擎与通用码之间的双向对应，从而使通用编码的理论结果可直接应用于热力学引擎的最优性能分析。

Abstract: We consider information engines that interact with a heat bath at temperature $T$, a work reservoir, and a tape carrying an individual (deterministic) sequence $x$ over a finite alphabet $\X$. An engine is a cascade of autonomous ratchets with fixed energy levels and detailed--balance dynamics, each processing segments of $m$ tape cells and having finitely many internal states. We prove a converse bound on the extractable work in terms of the empirical block entropies of $x$,and show that it is achievable \emph{universally}, by engines whose energy levels do not depend on $x$. The maximal work per symbol is $kT[\ln|\X|-\bar H(x)]$, where $k$ is Boltzmann's constant and $\bar H(x)$ is the asymptotic normalized aligned block entropy of $x$, which equals its block Lempel--Ziv complexity, and the minimal work needed to erase $x$ is $kT\bar H(x)$ per symbol. Underlying these results is a two--way correspondence with universal coding: energy levels define a code, and a suitably operated cascade extracts $kT$ times $\ln|\X|$ per symbol minus the code length; conversely, a fluctuation identity shows that every engine fixed in advance defines a code. Hence, converses of universal coding apply verbatim to engines; e.g., the optimal redundancy of memoryless universal engines is given by the Shtarkov sum.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究活性物质范式模型——Vicsek模型（VM）中集体序的涌现机制，揭示内在（角）噪声与外在（矢量）噪声下涌现方式的本质差异：内在噪声下集体序以连续式方式涌现，而外在噪声下则表现为突变式涌现。通过非平衡景观-流理论与粗粒化映射方法，阐明了景观演化、非平衡通量与熵产生率在涌现中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型是研究活性物质集体序涌现的典范系统，但此前关于密度诱导的涌现是否在角噪声（内在）与矢量噪声（外在）之间存在差异，以及何种机制主导这些相变，尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，厘清两类噪声下涌现行为的差异及其动力学与热力学起源。

Result: 研究发现，内在噪声下集体序从无序态以连续式方式涌现，景观从单势阱连续演化为双势阱再回到单势阱；外在噪声下则表现为突变式涌现，双势阱景观在临界相变处突然出现。反时针非平衡通量维持全局圆周运动，为景观重塑提供驱动力。平均通量与熵产生率在涌现阈值附近急剧变化，并在共存区呈现显著峰值，被确认为涌现的动力学与热力学起源。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了外在噪声下独特的突变式涌现，提供了更深入的机制洞见，并具有实际应用价值（如定位相边界、区分相变类型），且可推广至其他非平衡集体系统。

Abstract: Emergence of collective order from disordered constituents is a hallmark of active matter, and the Vicsek model (VM) serves as a paradigmatic system for its study. However, whether density-induced emergence in the VM differs between angular (intrinsic) and vectorial (extrinsic) noise, and what mechanisms govern the transitions, remain largely unexplored. Remarkably, we uncover that collective order emerges from a disordered state in a continuous-like manner under intrinsic noise but abruptly under extrinsic noise. Combining nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory with a coarse-grained mapping method, we show that the landscape evolves continuously from a single basin to two and back to one under intrinsic noise, but the two-basin landscape appears abruptly under extrinsic noise across the critical transition to flocking. The counterclockwise nonequilibrium flux sustains global circular motion, providing the driving force for reshaping the landscape. The mean flux and entropy production rate change steeply near the emergence threshold and exhibit pronounced peaks within the coexistence region, identifying them as the dynamical and thermodynamic origins of emergence. Our findings reveal a distinct abrupt emergence under extrinsic noise and offer deeper mechanistic insight, practical applications (e.g., locating phase boundaries and distinguishing transition types), and extensibility to other nonequilibrium collective systems.

</details>


### [20] [Rotation curves of dwarf galaxies and dark matter as a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons: reconstruction and fits of the deformation profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33999)
*Mahnaz Maleki,Asef Kheirandish,Morteza Nattagh Najafi,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由q变形玻色子构成的暗物质BEC晕，发现在Thomas-Fermi极限下均匀变形不改变标准BEC密度剖面，但位置依赖的变形能在内区压低χ参数，从而更好地拟合矮星系旋转曲线，多数情况下优于标准凝聚体、NFW和Burkert模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系由暗物质主导，是检验自相互作用BEC暗物质模型的敏感场所。标准BEC晕在Thomas-Fermi极限下密度剖面固定为ρ∝sin(kr)/(kr)，旋转曲线形状仅由两个尺度参数决定，拟合矮星系旋转曲线残差较大。作者希望引入q变形玻色子凝聚体，探究变形能否改进对观测旋转曲线的拟合，从而为暗物质性质提供新的约束。

Method: 在Thomas-Fermi极限下推导q变形玻色子凝聚体的流体静力学方程，发现空间均匀的变形参数只通过化学势进入晕结构，不改变标准BEC剖面。随后考虑位置依赖的变形参数，用两个附加参数的高斯函数描述参数χ(与q相关)在内区(kr≲1)的压低，并用该模型拟合矮星系旋转曲线，与标准凝聚体、NFW和Burkert剖面比较，采用贝叶斯信息准则(BIC)进行模型选择。

Result: 均匀变形不改变标准BEC密度剖面；位置依赖变形在kr≲1的内区压低χ，外区近似常数，标准凝聚体描述外晕、核心需q更接近1的较弱变形。所有星系剖面具有相似的定性形状，但压低的深度和宽度不普适。用高斯双参数描述压低后，旋转曲线拟合rms残差为0.6–2.6 km/s，而标准凝聚体为1.6–8.5 km/s。按BIC，变形凝聚体在4个星系中优于标准凝聚体、NFW和Burkert剖面，在2个星系中与最优者不可区分，在2个星系中被排除。

Conclusion: q变形玻色子凝聚体在Thomas-Fermi极限下通过位置依赖的变形参数改进了对矮星系旋转曲线的拟合，为暗物质自相互作用模型提供了新的参数化自由度。但结果受限于数据缺乏误差棒、忽略重子物质以及位置依赖变形参数的唯象地位，需进一步研究以验证其物理合理性。

Abstract: Dark-matter-dominated dwarf galaxies are a sensitive testing ground for models in which dark matter is a self-interacting Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC). In the Thomas-Fermi limit the standard BEC halo has the density profile $ρ\propto\sin(kr)/(kr)$, so that the shape of its rotation curve is fixed up to two scale parameters. Here we consider a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons. We show that, in the Thomas-Fermi limit, the deformation enters the structure of the halo only through the chemical potential of the condensate; consequently, a spatially uniform deformation parameter leaves the standard BEC profile unchanged. In every galaxy $χ$ is depressed in the inner region, $kr\lesssim1$, relative to the outer halo, where it is approximately constant in most cases: the standard condensate describes the outer halo, while the core requires a weaker deformation, i.e., $q$ closer to unity. The profiles share this qualitative shape, but neither the depth nor the width of the depression is universal. Modelling the depression by a Gaussian, with two additional parameters, we fit the rotation curves with rms residuals of $0.6$--$2.6$~km\,s$^{-1}$, against $1.6$--$8.5$~km\,s$^{-1}$ for the standard condensate. According to the Bayesian information criterion, the deformed condensate is preferred over the standard condensate, the Navarro-Frenk-White profile, and the Burkert profile in four galaxies, is indistinguishable from the best of them in two, and is disfavoured in two. We discuss the limitations of these results, in particular the absence of error bars in the data, the neglect of baryons, and the phenomenological status of a position-dependent deformation.

</details>

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry (TRS) breaking is the hallmark of non-equilibrium statistical physics. This notwithstanding, in translationally invariant systems, it is almost always treated only as a global quantity. We propose to use the local entropy production rate as a spatio-temporal field to describe extended homogeneous non-equilibrium systems. We find that this field generically displays spatial correlations, with a typical length that grows as we approach some non-equilibrium phase transitions. We also show that the correlation length associated with the local entropy production rate follows heretofore unknown scaling laws involving control parameters as well as the observation time used for trajectories. Our work paves the way to the use of the local entropy production as a powerful observable to identify the scale and source of TRS breaking in non-equilibrium systems.

Abstract: Interface roughness characterizes the boundary fluctuations across different length scales, which can reveal information about a material''s pinning landscape and collective interfacial behaviour. Finite sampling and image resolution can bias the measured scaling properties, however. These effects can particularly complicate comparisons between circular domains of different sizes, where the number of resolved boundary points, and the relationship between angular and physical separations vary with the interface radius. Here, we benchmark the recovery of the roughness exponent and amplitude using synthetic self-affine interfaces generated by Fourier synthesis, mapped onto circular contours, and rasterized onto a pixel grid. We first compare displacement-correlation (B(r)) and structure-factor (S(q)) estimates for different interface lengths. To address non-uniform angular and radial spacing in a circular interface, we assess a reconstruction procedure that weights slope minimization against medial interface position. We find that while spectral estimates were most effective in the original interfaces, this advantage did not persist after circular mapping. Moderate smoothing was found to improve parameter recovery, but stronger smoothing can bias both the scaling exponent and roughness amplitude. These results, verified on both synthetic datasets, and an experimental ferroelectric system, identify limitations of circular-interface roughness measurements and provide a basis for further investigations into the evolution of circular domains under finite resolution experiments.

Abstract: We introduce a data-enhanced self-learning projective quantum Monte Carlo (PQMC) method that incorporates projective measurements from programmable quantum simulators into the guiding wavefunction of a ground-state projection. We demonstrate the approach for a two-dimensional Rydberg-atom array using restricted Boltzmann machine (RBM) and recurrent neural network (RNN) guiding wavefunctions. Experimental pretraining reduces finite-population effects and accelerates self-learning convergence, allowing unbiased estimation of ground-state properties to be obtained with fewer walkers and fewer self-learning iterations. The improvement is particularly pronounced for the RBM, while the more accurate RNN guiding wavefunction exhibits a smaller, though still observable, benefit from pretraining. Simulations across different system sizes suggest that the relative advantage of experimental pretraining may increase with system size. Improvements are also observed for the checkerboard order parameter, despite deviations of the experimental measurements from the equilibrium numerical results. Finally, finite-size scaling with self-learning PQMC without experimental pretraining yields the critical point and critical exponents of the disordered-to-checkerboard transition in agreement with previous numerical studies. Our results show that limited and imperfect experimental measurements can provide an effective initialization for self-learning PQMC, while imaginary-time projection combined with subsequent unsupervised learning progressively refines the guiding wavefunction toward the ground state.

Abstract: Entanglement asymmetry has emerged as a powerful probe of symmetry restoration and the quantum Mpemba effect. However, its conventional Rényi generalizations, defined as differences of Rényi entropies, are not monotones in the resource-theoretic sense. Here, we investigate an alternative family of asymmetry quantifiers based on sandwiched Rényi divergences, which satisfy monotonicity under symmetry-preserving quantum channels. Developing a two-parameter replica approach, we show how these quantities can be computed from charged moments and establish a remarkable correspondence with conventional Rényi asymmetries under the inversion of the Rényi index, $α\leftrightarrow 1/α$. This relation, exact for pure states, holds at leading order in the relevant scaling limits for homogeneous quantum quenches, including interacting integrable systems, and for Haar-random states. Applying these results to quenches from tilted ferromagnetic states, we demonstrate that the quantum Mpemba effect is remarkably robust, occurring throughout the entire family of sandwiched Rényi asymmetry monotones. We identify dynamical regimes in quenches from spatially inhomogeneous initial states where conventional Rényi asymmetries and genuine monotones exhibit qualitatively different behaviours. We also investigate a solvable toy model illustrating the possible dependence of Mpemba crossings on the Rényi index. Our findings establish a connection between conventional Rényi asymmetries and quantum resource theory, explaining their effectiveness in broad classes of nonequilibrium protocols while highlighting the additional information provided by genuine asymmetry monotones.

</details>

Abstract: Recent experiments on superconducting heterostructures formed by an $s$-wave superconductor and a Dirac semimetal suggest that in these systems the superconducting state can break time-reversal symmetry even though no magnetic order or unconventional pairing is present in any of the structure's components. We present a minimal mechanism for a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconducting state in multiband systems proximity-coupled to a conventional $s$-wave superconductor. We derive the conditions for this state, find that it is the ground state wherever it exists, and obtain the phase diagrams. We find that in some situations the phase diagrams describe reentrant behavior: by continuously varying a parameter the system can undergo multiple transitions between a time-reversal-symmetric state and a state that spontaneously breaks time-reversal symmetry. The results help identify heterostructures that can be engineered to obtain nonreciprocal superconducting transport effects even when no magnetic field or magnetization is present.

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. Abstract: Schwinger-Dyson equations are a cornerstone of quantum field theory (QFT). In zero-dimensional QFTs with polynomial actions, which is just a physical interpretation for multivariate Gaussian integrals with polynomial insertions, conventional Gröbner elimination can be used to express all higher-order correlators (moments) in terms of a fixed basis set. One consequence is a closed finite-dimensional system of differential equations for the coupling dependence of these `master' correlators.

Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线自旋密度泛函理论需要超越局域自旋密度近似（LSDA）的泛函来刻画磁化强度的横向（方位角）变化，尤其是旋转梯度带来的能量贡献。然而缺乏严格的定量误差分析，难以评估这类近似的可靠性。作者受 Eich 和 Gross 工作的启发，希望以自旋螺旋波为局域参考构造近似并给出误差界。

Method: 作者采用定轴自旋螺旋波（SSW）作为提取 Hartree 项后的巨正则 Levy-Lieb 能量的局域参考：先构造总密度恒定、磁化强度为给定 SSW 的电子气的热力学极限，得到其单位体积能量 e_SSW；再以局域波矢 ∇φ（φ 为螺旋相位）处对该能量求值，形成泛函 E(ρ,m)≈∫ e_SSW(ρ(x),m(x),∇φ(x))dx。此处 ∇φ 度量 m 绕第三轴的局域旋转，在 m₁²+m₂²>0 时等于 (m₁∇m₂-m₂∇m₁)/(m₁²+m₂²)。当旋转坐标系中的自旋极化密度与局域波矢在 N^{1/3} 尺度上缓变时，通过渐近分析证明误差为 O(N^{5/6})。

Result: 得到了定轴 TSGA 的严格误差界：总能量为 O(N) 时误差为 O(N^{5/6})。近似泛函仅依赖于总密度 ρ、磁化强度 m 及其一阶导数。将相位 φ 置零后，该近似还原为非共线 LSDA，适用于任意磁化方向。

Conclusion: 本文为定轴横向自旋梯度近似建立了首个严格的定量误差界，表明以自旋螺旋波为局域参考的 TSGA 在缓变条件下具有可控精度，并统一了非共线 LSDA 作为其特例，为非共线自旋密度泛函理论的发展提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[
  E(ρ,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(ρ(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $ρ$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. Setting the phase to zero gives the noncollinear local spin density approximation (LSDA) for arbitrary magnetization directions.

</details>


### [43] [Backpropagation as Dirac-bracket dynamics of dissipative training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34479)
*Nicolaus Linneu Holanda,Ivan Eugenio Cunha*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将梯度下降、重球法与 Nesterov 加速梯度统一表述为哈密顿系统，证明反向传播是约束动力学的 Dirac 演化，并据此实现无需反向传播的网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为参数模型的梯度训练赋予一个物理（哈密顿/约束动力学）描述，从而把反向传播、自动微分和各类优化器统一到一个严格的几何/动力学框架中，并揭示网络层的前向传播本质上是一类约束（运动学链）。

Method: 采用 Bateman-Galley 自由度倍增（将耗散系统转化为带作用量原理的系统），把梯度流、重球法和 Nesterov 加速梯度写成哈密顿系统；将前馈网络各层激活视为相空间坐标、层间方程视为约束。利用这些约束对所有架构都是第二类约束，构造 Dirac 括号并用其哈密顿方程实现训练。约束矩阵的逆同时编码前向与反向模式自动微分，反向传播误差即层约束的拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 证明在该哈密顿描述下反向传播就是 Dirac 演化；哈密顿量只含网络输出的代价，但 Dirac 流精确再现损失的全梯度。激活对参数动量的 Dirac 括号即其 Jacobian；Nesterov 前瞻额外引入一层与动量相关的约束，耦合倍增系统的两个副本；梯度流可通过分阶段构造 Dirac 括号得到。数值验证达到机器精度，并成功通过积分约束的 Dirac 流训练网络，计算全程无需反向传播。

Conclusion: 梯度训练可完全纳入约束哈密顿力学框架，反向传播、自动微分与不同优化器均获得统一物理解释；该框架在完整倍增相空间上成立，并可替代传统反向传播进行实际训练。

Abstract: Gradient-based training of a parametric model is the motion of a damped particle in the potential set by the loss. Using the Bateman-Galley doubling of the degrees of freedom, which gives dissipative systems an action principle, we write gradient flow, the heavy-ball method and Nesterov's accelerated gradient method as Hamiltonian systems. We show that, within this Hamiltonian description, backpropagation is Dirac evolution. The activations of a feed-forward network are treated as coordinates of phase space, and the layer equations as constraints. These constraints are second class for every architecture, and Hamilton's equations with the resulting Dirac bracket reproduce training with the exact gradient of the loss, although the Hamiltonian contains only the cost of the network output. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. These results hold in the full doubled phase space, not only in the physical limit. Abstract: We study the large-$N$ behavior of the optimal constant $κ_N$ in the two-dimensional fermionic many-particle Hardy inequality \[ \sum_{i=1}^N -Δ_i \geq κ_N^{(2)}\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq N}\frac{1}{|x_i-x_j|^2}, \] understood as a quadratic-form inequality on antisymmetric wave functions. A trace inequality controls the low-occupation regime, while sums of Neumann eigenvalues yield the sharp bound in the high-occupation regime.

Abstract: It has been emphasized recently that some extremal black holes are "critical" solutions, lying at the threshold between gravitational collapse and dispersion. Near criticality, this Hamiltonian is shown to be canonically equivalent to that of a Liouville theory with negative potential. After quantization, this model predicts that a collapsing shell has a non-trivial probability of undergoing a quantum mechanical bounce when its energy above extremality is comparable to the "breakdown scale". The bounce probability approaches unity at extremality. Hence this model predicts that the critical surface in the classical theory is thickened quantum mechanically into a "critical layer". The probability density function for the black hole energy is suppressed below the breakdown scale in the same way as found recently by Turiaci and Wu.

Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

Abstract: In this work, we study local operator quenches in thermal two-dimensional conformal field theories with complex-time evolution, which can be viewed as an effective description of conditional dynamics in a system interacting with an environment. Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. We derive bounds on Love numbers and quasinormal modes from constraints on the impedance that follow from either of two properties of a stable body: it is lossless, meaning that it dissipates no energy, or it is passive, meaning that it never gives back more energy than it has received. For a lossless body, every dynamical Love number is bounded from above and below, exactly in general relativity, in any renormalization scheme and at any scale. Earlier bounds are exact for Newtonian stars but fail for compact stars, because they take the response to arbitrarily fast tides to be zero or non-negative, whereas in general relativity it is negative. The quasinormal modes of a lossless body are also excluded from a region of the complex frequency plane set by the mass and radius. Each known Love number enlarges the excluded region. Conversely, the quasinormal modes bound each Love number from below, given the lower ones. For a passive body of horizon scale, such as a black hole, the static Love number lies in a window that closes onto the vanishing black-hole value as the body's surface approaches the horizon.

The extension to include minimally coupled gravity is straightforward. Examples are provided, including constructing the Dirac equation in spherical and cylindrical symmetry (with or without gravity) and the Kerr spacetime. We also briefly comment on the advantages and disadvantages of various tetrads and orthonormal gamma matrices. This manuscript should be accessible to readers who have studied the Dirac equation at an introductory level (e.g., in Cartesian coordinates in flat spacetime with constant gamma matrices). The emphasis is on the use of orthonormal frames, since these correspond to the coordinate systems most familiar to readers when dealing with most field theories, and represent the physical frames where measurements are performed. Abstract: We outline the history of the research of dynamic systems including higher derivatives in the Lagrangian, The story starts in 1744 when Euler published a book where the Euler--Lagrange differential equations for a HD system were first written. The next important landmarks were the papers of Ostrogradsky (1850) and of Pais and Uhlenbeck (1950). Many articles written in the second half of the last century were influenced by a misconception that was widespread at the time --- it was believed that higher derivatives necessarily bring about a violation of unitarity.
  We understand today that this is not the case and there are many systems with benign ghosts where HD are present but unitarity is not violated. In this review, we present and discuss several examples of such (quantum) mechanical systems and 2D field theories. Then we will briefly discuss higher-derivative gravity theories in 4 dimensions. In that case, the question whether the ghosts there are benign or not has a special status due to the absence of a nonperturbative Hamiltonian and does not have a definite answer yet. However, if one restricts oneself to classical theory, one can argue that the ghosts {\it are} benign (benign classical ghostly system is a system that needs an infinite time to reach a singularity, and it seems to the case both in ordinary and in HD gravity: the cosmic censorship conjecture implies that the singularities are always surrounded by horizons, and it takes an infinite time on the clocks of a distant observer to reach a horizon).
  Finally, I discuss supersymmetric HD theories: conformal supergravity, where the ghosts are also probably benign, and then an interesting supersymmetric HD gauge theory in 6 dimensions. It involves a dimensionless coupling constant, is renormalizable and rather beautiful. Unfortunately, it is not unitary --- ghosts are not benign there.

</details>

Abstract: We study late-time out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) of semi-degenerate primary fields in two-dimensional $A_{N-1}$ Toda conformal field theory. In Liouville theory ($N=2$), we find that the OTOC decays as $t^{-3/2}$ when the continuum edge dominates, correcting a previously reported constant limit. For $N=3$, a transverse contour pinch reduces the spectral integral to one dimension. Assuming that the reduced expansion is complete, in the continuous phase the regulated edge behaves as a discrete state, and the OTOC approaches a pure braiding phase. As we check numerically, the edge block is a free-field correlator in a charge-neutral Weyl frame; this explains the pure phase and is consistent with crossing symmetry. For both $N=2$ and $N=3$, a dominant discrete residue gives a constant late-time limit. For $N\geq4$, the same one-dimensional reduction violates a crossing bound on the lowest intermediate weight of a positive diagonal expansion. Hence the $N=2,3$ phase diagram does not extend to higher rank without additional contributions below the reduced continuum edge or outside a positive diagonal expansion.

Method: 在弱耦合共形规范理论中，考虑由标量算符在最低阶产生两个部分子的态，计算两个时间加权探测器（光射线算子 ∫du u Tuu）在分离方向上的关联函数；通过双圈计算识别发散来源，分析软部分子对的贡献并给出任意物质内容下的角度依赖闭式系数，在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论中利用已知四点函数的重胶子 Regge 项恢复该系数并关联电荷关联函数中的对数，进而在平面极限下用 BFKL 截距对主导对数进行重求和。

Result: 双圈层次两个时间加权能量探测器的关联函数出现以时间窗口对数形式发散；该对数并非来自单个软胶子，而是来自能量相当的软部分子对（每个探测器各一个部分子），其系数对任意物质内容给出角度依赖的闭式表达式。在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论中，该系数可由已知四点函数的两胶子 Regge 项恢复，并与电荷关联函数中的对数建立简单关系；平面理论中主导对数可重求和为时间窗口的幂次，幂次由 BFKL 截距决定。

Conclusion: 时间加权使能量关联函数在规范理论中红外发散，其双圈对数来自每个探测器各一的软部分子对。该系数在 N=4 理论中与四点函数的 Regge 项及电荷关联函数对数直接关联，平面极限下主导对数由 BFKL 截距控制并可重求和为时间窗口的幂次。

Abstract: Energy correlators are infrared safe because each detector weights a parton by its energy, but a weight in the arrival time makes them infrared unsafe in gauge theories. For the state created by a scalar operator that makes two partons at lowest order in a weakly coupled conformal gauge theory, we show that the correlator of two time-weighted energy detectors (the light-ray operators $\int\!du\,u\,T_{uu}$) at separated directions diverges at two loops like the logarithm of the time window. The logarithm does not come from a single soft gluon, but from a soft parton pair with comparable energies, one parton in each detector; its coefficient is a closed-form function of the angle for any matter content. In $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, we recover this coefficient from the two-gluon Regge term of the known four-point function and derive a simple relation to the logarithm in the charge correlator. In the planar theory the leading logarithms resum to a power of the window, set by the BFKL intercept.

</details>


### [88] [Massive Triangle in Cosmological Correlator beyond Conformal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34892)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了大质量标量与暴胀子成对耦合所产生的一圈原初双谱（bispectrum）的两个领头质量修正的闭合解析表达式，并用独立全圈计算验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前，在一般动量下，超出共形耦合情形的大质量标量圈图对原初双谱的质量修正缺乏闭合解析表达式，限制了理论预测与观测对比。

Method: 作者引入辅助能量（auxiliary-energy）修饰来保持有理时间积分，从而在任意外部动量下将两个领头质量修正表示为均匀权（uniform weight）三和四的多重对数（multiple polylogarithms）；并通过包含盲测预言的独立全圈评估进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到了两个领头质量修正的闭合形式，揭示了尽管存在椭圆中间几何，结果仍具有意想不到的简洁性；同时确定了一个次领头轻场奇异性，并恢复（recover）了重质量有效场论。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在任意动量下给出大质量圈图双谱质量修正的闭合解析表达式，克服了共形耦合的限制，并为相关原初非高斯性计算提供了验证手段与有效理论衔接。

Abstract: A massive scalar coupled in pairs to the inflaton generates a one-loop primordial bispectrum whose mass corrections at generic momenta have lacked closed analytic expressions beyond conformal coupling. We obtain two leading mass corrections at arbitrary external momenta in terms of multiple polylogarithms of uniform weights three and four. Auxiliary-energy dressings preserve rational time integrals, revealing unexpected simplicity despite elliptic intermediate geometry. Independent full-loop evaluations, including blinded predictions, validate the closed forms. We also determine a subleading light-field singularity and recover the heavy-mass effective theory.

</details>
