### [89] [On the binding and stability of CeO2 molecules on low-index Au surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33338)
*Ying-Cheng Chen,Ahmad Ahmad,Anter El-Azab*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋极化密度泛函理论研究了 CeO2 分子在 Au(001)、(110) 和 (111) 表面的结合与稳定性，发现弯曲构型的 CeO2 比线性构型更稳定，并根据结合方式将 CeO2-Au 相互作用归纳为化学吸附对称键、化学吸附非对称键和物理吸附对称键三种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自组装生长的多相薄膜涉及金属相与氧化物相的共沉积，此时氧化物可能以化学计量分子形式存在于金属表面。量化这类分子氧化物态在金属表面上的稳定性、结合与扩散对理解自组装至关重要，而现有知识对 CeO2 分子在 Au 表面的行为尚不充分。

Method: 采用自旋极化密度泛函理论计算，系统考察 CeO2 分子在 Au(001)、Au(110)、Au(111) 表面不同结合位点上的构型、结合能、结合自由能、结构稳定性、有效电荷及投影电子态密度。

Result: 分子态 CeO2 在所有 Au 表面上均比其原子组分更有利；弯曲构型比线性构型更稳定，最优构型为 Ce 原子占据 hollow 位、O 原子占据 top 位。结合能约为或弱于 -1 eV 的构型在室温以上存在概率极低。电荷转移使 CeO2 在所有表面上都发生极化，但仅在 Au(001) 和 Au(110) 的强键合态中极化更显著。确定了三种结合情形：情形 1 化学吸附对称键、情形 2 化学吸附非对称键、情形 3 物理吸附对称键；Au(001) 和 Au(110) 上存在全部三种情形，而 Au(111) 上仅存在情形 2 和 3。

Conclusion: CeO2 分子在 Au 各表面上的稳定结合以弯曲构型为主，其结合强度足以区分化学吸附与物理吸附，并呈现出表面取向依赖的结合模式差异，这些结果为理解金属-氧化物共沉积体系的自组装行为提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Many multi-phase thin films grown by self-assembly involve co-deposition of both metallic and oxide phases that grow into the same film. Under such conditions, the oxide species may be present in stoichiometric molecular form on the surface of the metallic phase. Quantifying the stability, binding, and diffusion of such molecular oxide states on metallic surfaces is critical for understanding self-assembly. In this work, we investigate the binding and stability of CeO2 molecules on Au surfaces using spin-polarized density functional theory calculations. Specifically, we examine how the configuration of the CeO2 molecule, its binding energy and binding free energy, structural stability, effective charge, and projected electronic density of states vary among various binding sites on Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) surfaces. Molecular CeO2 was found to be more favorable on all Au surfaces than its atomic constituents. It was also found that the bent CeO2 molecule configuration is more stable than the linear counterpart, with the Ce atom on the hollow site and the O atoms on the top sites. In addition, the configurations of the CeO2 molecule on Au surfaces with a binding energy around or weaker than -1 eV were found to have a low probability of existence above room temperature. Furthermore, the charge transfer was found to occur in such a way that the CeO2 molecule becomes polarized on all surfaces, but the polarization is stronger only for the strongly bonded states on Au(001) and Au(110) surfaces. Finally, three different cases of how the CeO2 molecule binds to Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) were identified: case 1: chemisorption with symmetric bonds, case 2: chemisorption with asymmetric bonds, and case 3: physisorption with symmetric bonds. These three cases of CeO2 binding are present on Au(001) and Au(110), but only cases 2 and 3 occur on Au(111).

</details>


### [90] [Magnetically Guided Sequential Multi-Filler Alignment Enables Anisotropic Heat Pathways in Stretchable Composites for Soft Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33381)
*Altynay Kaidarova,Viktor Naenen,Isidro Florenciano,Ruben Windey,Xiaoyan Qing,Laurens Snels,Juhyung Park,Tom Van der Donck,Jeroen Soete,David Seveno,Francisco Molina-Lopez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层级两步工艺策略，通过“先排列后共混”构建镍微粒垂直导热骨架，再用液态金属/镍混合物填充间隙，制得兼具高面外导热（7.5 W/(m·K)）、极低面内电导（<10⁻¹² S/m）和超软机械顺应性（约160 kPa）的可拉伸热界面复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可拉伸、皮肤贴合电子器件需要同时具备高面外导热、机械柔软和面内电绝缘的热界面材料，但颗粒填充复合材料中这些需求通常是相互矛盾的：提高颗粒含量缩短颗粒间距以增强导热时，不可避免地带来机械刚化和面内导电。现有的颗粒排列加工策略难以有效解耦这些多重功能。

Method: 采用层级两步工艺：（1）“先排列后共混”——在空气中通过磁场排列镍（Ni）微粒形成骨架，再注入聚合物，由于排列阶段无粘性弹性体基体，可处理大量颗粒并组装成高度有序、致密的垂直导热通路；（2）将液态金属（LM）/镍混合物渗入已排列的骨架以桥接颗粒间间隙，降低颗粒间热阻。

Result: 所得复合材料实现7.5 W/(m·K)的面外导热系数，面内电导率低于10⁻¹² S/m，且具备约160 kPa的超软机械顺从性。该材料成功用作热电发电器中的耦合层以及可穿戴技术的手指贴合散热垫。

Conclusion: 通过层级两步工艺可有效诱导软复合材料产生强导热各向异性，同时实现高面外导热、面内电绝缘与超软力学性能的解耦，为可穿戴电子和热电应用提供了高性能热界面材料解决方案。

Abstract: Stretchable, skin-conformal electronics demand thermal interface materials that combine high through-plane heat transport with mechanical softness and lateral electrical insulation. In particle-filled composites, these requirements are typically antagonistic. Heat transport is governed not only by the intrinsic thermal conductivity of the fillers but, more critically, by thermal resistance at the interparticle interface. As the interparticle distance is reduced by increasing the particle content, mechanical reinforcement and in-plane electrical conductivity become unavoidable. The existing particle alignment processing strategies result in inefficient uncoupling of multifunctionality. Here, we present a hierarchical two-step process strategy to induce strong conductivity anisotropy in a soft composite. First, an align-before-blend process forms a scaffold of magnetically aligned nickel (Ni) microparticles in air before polymer infiltration. The absence of the viscous elastomer matrix during the particle alignment facilitates handling large amounts of particles, which assemble into highly ordered and densely packed vertical heat-conduction pathways. Second, a liquid metal (LM)/Ni mixture is infiltrated into the aligned scaffold to bridge interparticle gaps, minimizing interparticle thermal resistance. The composite reaches 7.5 W/(m K) through-plane thermal conductivity with lateral electrical conductivity below $10^{-12}$ S/m and ultrasoft mechanical compliance (approximately 160 kPa), enabling coupling layers in thermoelectric generators and finger-mounted heat-spreading pads for wearable technologies.

</details>


### [91] [Unveiling ferroelectricity in wafer-scale beta-phase In$_2$Se$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33405)
*Kyungmin Yang,Hyunmin Cho,Jihwan Jeon,Tae Hyeon Park,Seojeong Ro,Hye Jung Kim,Jung-Hoon Lee,Jaewon Wang,Hyeon Suk Shin,Young-Min Kim,Chang Woo Myung,Roy H. Olsson,Seunguk Song,Deep Jariwala*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现二维范德华半导体 β-In₂Se₃ 在单分子五层内存在皮米级畸变诱导的双稳态极性序，可在超薄极限（双层约1.9 nm）下实现垂直电场开关的铁电性，并展示了其在电容器阵列和铁电半导体晶体管中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三维铁电体在减薄时因退极化场和界面效应而失去铁电性，因此将铁电性推进到极限厚度是存储器硬件的核心挑战。作者旨在寻找一种能在超薄尺度维持可开关极性的材料。

Method: 研究者选用此前被认为中心对称的范德华半导体 β-In₂Se₃，发现每个五原子层（quintuple layer）内的皮米级结构畸变产生具有面外和面内分量的极性态。通过金属有机物化学气相沉积（MOCVD）在2英寸晶圆上生长薄膜，并利用垂直电场驱动极性翻转，测试了双层极限下的矫顽场、剩余极化、器件一致性、耐疲劳性和保持特性。

Result: β-In₂Se₃ 的极性态在双层（约1.9 nm）极限下仍可开关，估计矫顽场为0.9 MV/cm，表观剩余极化为11.0 μC/cm²。2英寸晶圆上制备的电容器阵列在100个器件间开关电压离散度低于5%，耐疲劳性达10⁷次循环，预测十年保持特性良好。由于极性态与半导体能隙共存，同一薄膜可同时作为电容器的开关层和铁电半导体晶体管的沟道。

Conclusion: β-In₂Se₃ 是一类兼具超薄铁电性和半导体特性的范德华材料，突破了传统铁电体的厚度限制，为实现高密度、可集成的铁电存储与逻辑一体化器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: Pushing ferroelectricity to the ultimate thickness limit is a central challenge for memory hardware, because conventional three-dimensional ferroelectrics degrade through depolarization and interfacial effects when thinned. Here we identify $β$-In$_2$Se$_3$, a van der Waals semiconductor regarded as centrosymmetric, as a host of bistable polar order arising from a picometre-scale distortion within each quintuple layer. The polar state has out-of-plane and in-plane components, is switched by a vertical electric field, and persists to the bilayer limit (~1.9 nm) with an estimated coercive field of 0.9 MV/cm and an apparent remanent polarization of 11.0 $μ$C/cm$^2$. Films grown by metal-organic chemical vapor deposition on 2-inch wafers yield capacitor arrays with a switching-voltage spread below 5% across 100 devices, an endurance of $10^7$ cycles and a projected ten-year retention. Because the polar state coexists with a semiconducting gap, one film serves as both the switching layer of a capacitor and the channel of a ferroelectric semiconductor transistor.

</details>


### [92] [Electron--phonon interactions in a pseudo--atomic orbital basis: superconductivity, transport, spectroscopy and thermal emission from one interpolated vertex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33473)
*Marco Buongiorno Nardelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于赝原子轨道（pseudo-atomic orbitals）固定基组的无参数电子-声子耦合计算方法，已在开源代码 PAOFLOW 中实现，并通过对铅和硅的验证表明其精度与传统 Wannier 函数方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用对超导、电导率、光吸收和激发寿命等物性至关重要。传统第一性原理计算需要用最大局域化 Wannier 函数将耦合插值到密集布里渊区网格，但该方法依赖材料特定的手动调参，难以实现高通量自动化计算。

Method: 采用从赝势自动生成的赝原子轨道固定基组（无需手动调节），在开源代码 PAOFLOW 中实现电子-声子顶点的插值。耦合可由密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）或位移超胞计算获得，并纳入长程场效应与自旋轨道耦合。

Result: 在相同输入下，该方法计算的电子-声子顶点与成熟代码结果吻合。在铅上复现了声子色散、超导能隙、临界温度、电阻率、光电子能谱扭结和回旋质量；在硅上复现了声子辅助光吸收、晶圆发射率和载流子迁移率，精度与 Wannier 方法相当。

Conclusion: 该无参数框架可实现大规模材料数据库的高通量、自动化电子-声子计算，为第一性原理电子-声子耦合研究提供了免调参的通用工具。

Abstract: Electron--phonon interactions govern superconductivity, electrical resistivity, optical absorption and electronic excitation lifetimes in solids. First--principles calculations require interpolating this coupling onto dense Brillouin--zone grids, traditionally using maximally localized Wannier functions that demand material--specific tuning. Here we show that a fixed basis of pseudo--atomic orbitals--automatically generated from pseudopotentials and implemented in the open--source code PAOFLOW captures the electron--phonon vertex with equivalent accuracy and no manual tuning. Computed from density--functional perturbation theory or displaced supercells, and incorporating long--range fields and spin--orbit coupling, the vertex matches established codes on identical inputs. We validate the method on lead, reproducing its phonon dispersion, superconducting gap, critical temperature, resistivity, photoemission kink and cyclotron masses, and on silicon, capturing phonon--assisted optical absorption, wafer emissivity and carrier mobilities. This parameter--free framework enables high--throughput, automated electron--phonon calculations across large materials databases.

</details>


### [93] [Low-barrier-height Hf$_\text{1-x}$Zr$_\text{x}$O$_2$ ferroelectric tunnel junctions with large ON-state currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33495)
*Shaoqing Ding,Jinyuan Yao,Eknath Sarkar,Emmanuel Quezada,Thomas N. Jackson,Susan Trolier-McKinstry,Suman Datta,Ying Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了以超薄 Hf₁₋ₓZrₓO₂（HZO，≤5nm）为势垒的铁电隧道结（FTJ）在低至 2 K 温度下的电输运机制，发现低温下以直接量子隧穿为主并伴随陷阱辅助隧穿，高温则由热激活导通主导；据此建立的极简模型揭示势垒高度显著低于能带排列预期，并归因于浅陷阱带的形成，为同时实现大开态电流、高隧穿电阻比和温度稳定性提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电隧道结（FTJ）在非易失存储、神经形态计算等领域前景广阔，而 HZO 因与 CMOS 工艺兼容成为势垒材料首选。近期研究强调，要兑现 FTJ 的技术承诺，必须深入理解电子穿过铁电势垒的输运机制，这是本工作的核心动因。

Method: 制备势垒为超薄 HZO（≤5nm）的 FTJ，并将其电学特性测量至 2 K 的低温；通过温度依赖的输运分析区分直接量子隧穿、陷阱辅助隧穿和热激活导通的贡献；在此基础上构建一个能够解释全部实验现象的极简（minimal）模型，对势垒高度进行定量提取。

Result: 低温下输运由直接量子隧穿主导，并伴有陷阱辅助隧穿；随温度升高热激活导通渐占主导。极简模型揭示势垒高度显著低于能带排列图像所预测的值，这一意外结果被归因于浅陷阱带的出现。

Conclusion: 浅陷阱带的形成导致势垒高度显著降低，这揭示了一条此前未被探索的路径，有望在 FTJ 的高速存储应用中同时实现大开态电流、高隧穿电阻比（TER）以及改善的温度稳定性。

Abstract: Ferroelectric tunnel junctions (FTJs) hold great promise for nonvolatile memory, neuromorphic computing, and other applications. Hf$_\text{1-x}$Zr$_\text{x}$O$_2$ (HZO) has become a leading candidate for the tunnel barrier due primarily to its compatibility with CMOS technology. Recent advances on HZO based FTJs have highlighted the importance of understanding the electrical transport through a ferroelectric tunnel barrier for fulfilling the technological promises of FTJs. In this paper we report investigation of HZO FTJs featuring a barrier of ultrathin HZO ($\leq$5nm) measured down to a temperature of 2 K. Direct quantum tunneling was found to dominate at low temperatures, along with contributions from trap-assisted tunneling. As the temperature increases, conduction by thermal activation takes over. We developed a minimal model that accounts for all experimental observations, revealing a barrier height significantly lower than that of the band alignment picture, an unexpected finding attributable to the emergence of a shallow trap band. This finding reveals a previously unexplored pathway toward simultaneously achieving large ON-state currents, high tunnel electroresistance and improved temperature stability for the high-speed memory applications of FTJs.

</details>


### [94] [ILMlip: fast and universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33538)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Christian Dreßler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ILMlip 是一系列为速度和零样本精度同时优化的等变、保守消息传递原子间势，能以比现有 SOTA 模型快得多(约 10³ 倍于 MACE 等)的速度运行分子动力学，同时在 Matbench Discovery 上取得高达 0.91 的 F1 分数，逼近当前最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练的机器学习原子间势越来越准确，但模型越来越大、训练成本越来越高(当前 Matbench Discovery 领先模型有 9.9×10⁷ 参数、需 3600 GPU 小时)。作者希望获得兼具快速推理与零样本精度的势函数，以服务于高通量、大规模或长时间模拟，并作为微调的良好起点。

Method: ILMlip 采用等变、保守的消息传递势，每个组件都为吞吐量而设计：每对原子间计算尽可能少、每个原子上做所需的计算。论文提供 6 种尺寸(1.4×10⁵ 至 2.8×10⁷ 参数)，在材料、分子和固体酸上预训练，并通过 ASE 在单个 GPU 上进行 MD 模拟评估。

Result: 速度上，ILMlip 比 MACE(medium)等 SOTA 消息传递势快约 10³ 倍，比 NEP89 和 DPA-4.1C 快达 5 倍。精度上，在 Matbench Discovery 上 F1 分数达到 0.75–0.91，最大模型与当前领先者相差不到 0.03。微调到六种材料时，S 尺寸模型接近微调 MACE 的从头算力误差，在 Cu fcc 上达到约 50 ns/原子步的参考级精度。

Conclusion: ILMlip 在速度与零样本精度之间取得优秀平衡，适用于高通量、大尺度或长时间模拟，且因其可迁移性可作为微调的强起点。其组件可独立采用，作为构建更优速度–精度比架构的起点。实现已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained machine-learned interatomic potentials keep growing more accurate as new models appear at a rapid pace, and more expensive: the current leader of Matbench Discovery as of November 2026 (F1 score 0.935) has $9.9\times10^7$ parameters and took 3.6 thousand GPU-hours to train. The ILMlip potentials presented here are optimised for speed as well as zero-shot accuracy. They are equivariant, conservative message-passing potentials in which every component is arranged for throughput: little work per pair of atoms and as much as needed per atom. Through ASE on one GPU, they run molecular dynamics up to about $10^3$ times faster than state-of-the-art message-passing potentials such as MACE (medium) and up to five times faster than NEP89 and DPA-4.1C. At the same time, six sizes with $1.4\times10^5$ to $2.8\times10^7$ parameters, pretrained on materials, molecules and solid acids, reach F1 scores of 0.75 to 0.91 on Matbench Discovery, placing the largest within 0.03 of the current leader. They therefore suit high-throughput, large or long simulations, and, being transferable, they are strong starting points for fine-tuning. Fine-tuned to six materials, the model of size S comes close to the ab initio force errors of a fine-tuned MACE, so it reaches reference-level accuracy at about 50 ns per atom and step (Cu fcc). Their components can be adopted separately, as a starting point for architectures with even better ratios of speed to accuracy. The ILMlip implementation is available at https://github.com/jhaens/ilmlip

</details>


### [95] [Effects of Yttrium doping on Proton Conductivity in Ba(Fe, Co, Zr, Y)O$_{3-δ}$ Cathode Materials for Proton Ceramic Fuel Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33543)
*Chiyoung Kim,Ryan Jacobs,Jun Meng,Jack H. Duffy,Kyle S. Brinkman,Yueh-Lin Lee,Harry W. Abernathy,Dane Morgan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用 AIMD 模拟与热力学缺陷模型,系统研究了 Y 掺杂对 BCFZY 三重导电钙钛矿阴极质子电导率的影响,发现Y掺杂对质子扩散系数和浓度提升幅度有限,并揭示了气氛条件对质子传输机制(羟基 vs 氢化物)的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子陶瓷燃料电池(PCFCs)在中温下能量转换效率高,但其性能常受限于阴极迟缓的氧还原动力学。三重导电钙钛矿(如 BCFZY)是极具前景的空气电极材料,但 Y 掺杂对质子电导率的具体影响机制尚不清楚,尤其需要将质子扩散率与质子浓度两个效应解耦分析。

Method: 采用从头算分子动力学(AIMD)模拟评估三种组分(BCFZ、BCFZY0.1、BCFY)的质子示踪扩散系数;同时构建包含水合、氧化和氢化物生成反应的热力学缺陷模型,估算温度相关的质子浓度;在 PCFC 运行气氛与低氧分压条件下比较各组分质子电导率及主导传输机制。

Result: Y 含量从 BCFZ 增至 BCFY 时,500 °C 下质子示踪扩散系数提升 1.7%;在 PCFC 运行气氛下,质子浓度与质子电导率分别提升 3.8% 和 5.3%。这些提升幅度小于还原条件下实验中报道的显著增强,说明气氛条件对评估 Y 掺杂效应至关重要。低氧分压下,主导质子传输机制由羟基(OH_O•)转变为氢化物(H_O•)。

Conclusion: Y 掺杂对 BCFZY 正极质子传输的影响在氧化性 PCFC 气氛下较为温和,远小于还原条件下的报道。该工作从机理层面阐明了 BCFZY 阴极中的质子传输行为,并为下一代 PCFC 质子导电空气电极的优化提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Protonic ceramic fuel cells (PCFCs) offer efficient energy conversion at intermediate temperatures, but their performance is often constrained by sluggish oxygen reduction kinetics at the cathode. Triple conducting perovskite materials such as BaCo$_x$Fe$_y$Zr$_z$Y$_{1-x-y-z}$O$_{3-δ}$ (BCFZY) have shown promise as air electrodes. In this study, we investigated the impact of Y doping on proton conductivity by decoupling the effects on proton diffusivity and proton concentration. Using ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations, we assessed proton tracer diffusivity across three compositions: BaCo$_{0.4}$Fe$_{0.4}$Zr$_{0.2}$O$_{3-δ}$ (BCFZ), BaCo$_{0.4}$Fe$_{0.4}$Zr$_{0.1}$Y$_{0.1}$O$_{3-δ}$ (BCFZY0.1) and BaCo$_{0.4}$Fe$_{0.4}$Y$_{0.2}$O$_{3-δ}$ (BCFY). Thermodynamic defect models incorporating hydration, oxidation, and hydride formation reactions are employed to estimate temperature-dependent proton concentrations. Our results suggest that increasing the Y content from BCFZ to BCFY increases proton diffusivity by 1.7% at 500 $^\circ$C. Under PCFC operating gas conditions, proton concentration and proton conductivity are predicted to increase by 3.8% and 5.3%, respectively, at 500 $^\circ$C. These increases are modest compared with the larger enhancements reported experimentally under reducing conditions, highlighting the importance of gas conditions in evaluating the effects of Y doping. Under low oxygen partial pressure conditions, we predict that the dominant proton transport mechanism shifts from hydroxyl groups (OH$_O^{\bullet}$) to hydride species (H$_O^{\bullet}$). This work provides mechanistic insight into proton transport in BCFZY cathodes and offers design principles for optimizing proton-conducting air electrodes in next-generation PCFCs.

</details>


### [96] [Machine-Learning Spin-Fermion Models for Strongly Disordered Magnets: Sb-Doped MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33544)
*Do Hoon Kiem,Parul R. Raghuvanshi,David S. Parker,Lucas Lindsay,Valentino R. Cooper,Tom Berlijn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将第一性原理计算重整化到无序自旋-费米子模型上的机器学习方法，并将其应用于Sb掺杂的MnTe，证明交替磁性（altermagnetism）在约25% Sb掺杂下仍持续存在，并揭示无序展宽是调控自旋选择性的新因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属性交替磁体在自旋电子学中极具前景，但大多数交替磁体是绝缘的。掺杂可引入金属性，却同时带来难以用第一性原理直接建模的化学与磁无序，因此需要一种能同时处理无序与电子-磁耦合的方法。

Method: 提出一种机器学习方法，将第一性原理计算重整化为无序自旋-费米子模型；所学模型能准确复现第一性原理的磁性与电子性质，并能在大型无序超胞中预测数百万个非等价长程交换耦合。

Result: 将该方法应用于Sb掺杂的MnTe表明，交替磁性在约25% Sb掺杂下仍持续存在；此外，该方法揭示了无序展宽是调控自旋选择性的新因素。

Conclusion: 这项工作为将重整化与机器学习哈密顿量相结合以研究强无序材料开辟了新途径，并深化了对掺杂交替磁体中无序效应的理解。

Abstract: Metallic altermagnets are promising for spintronics, but most altermagnets are insulating. Doping can induce metallicity, but introduces chemical and magnetic disorder that is challenging to model from first principles. Here, we introduce a machine-learning approach that renormalizes first-principles calculations onto disordered spin-fermion models. The learned models accurately reproduce first-principles magnetic and electronic properties and enable predictions of millions of inequivalent long-range exchange couplings in large disordered supercells. Applied to Sb-doped MnTe, the approach shows that altermagnetism persists up to about 25\% Sb, and reveals disorder broadening as a new factor controlling spin selectivity. More broadly, our work opens a route to combining renormalization with machine-learned Hamiltonians for strongly disordered materials.

</details>


### [97] [Electron exchange matters in electron spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33637)
*Michael J. Landry,Chuhang Liu,Ashley S. Dale,L. Dagny Davis,Griffin M. Haisman,Lijun Wu,Ray F. Egerton,Yimei Zhu,Mingda Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相对论性磁振子(magnon)EELS理论，将磁偶极相互作用与电子交换相互作用同时纳入，证明两通道因电子自旋态可区分而在常规EELS中不相干，但自旋分辨探测可消除路径信息、显现一阶干涉，并产生无需出射自旋分析即可观测的左右强度不对称，可直接探测磁振子角动量并区分手性相反的激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EELS只考虑探针电子与材料电子之间的经典库仑相互作用，忽略了相对论能量下的费米子交换效应。动机是填补这一理论空缺，揭示电子交换相互作用在磁振子激发中的作用，并探索利用自旋相关干涉实现纳米尺度手性磁激发的测量。

Method: 发展包含磁偶极相互作用与电子交换相互作用两种散射通道的相对论磁振子EELS理论；分析两通道散射后电子自旋态的可区分性及其对干涉的影响；引入自旋分辨探测以消除路径信息，恢复一阶干涉；利用自旋极化的入射电子束研究散射强度的左右不对称性。

Result: 磁偶极与电子交换两通道使电子处于可区分的自旋态，导致常规EELS中二者干涉被抑制；自旋分辨探测消除路径信息后显现出弱交换振幅下的一阶干涉；该干涉导致自旋极化入射下出现左右强度不对称，无需出射自旋分析即可观测。

Conclusion: 该干涉信号直接探测磁振子角动量，并能区分手性相反的激发，为纳米尺度手性磁激发测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS) conventionally considers classical Coulomb interactions between probe and material electrons, overlooking their fermionic exchange at relativistic energies. Here, we develop a relativistic theory of magnon EELS incorporating both magnetic dipole and electron exchange interactions. We show that the two channels leave distinguishable electron spin states, suppressing their interference in conventional EELS. However, spin-resolved detection erases this which-path information, revealing first-order interference despite the weak exchange amplitude. Remarkably, the same interference produces a left-right intensity asymmetry with a spin-polarized incident beam, without requiring outgoing-spin analysis. We demonstrate that this signal directly probes magnon angular momentum and distinguishes excitations of opposite handedness, opening a route to nanoscale measurements of chiral magnetic excitations.

</details>


### [98] [Foundation interatomic potentials capture structural disorder in metallic glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34156)
*Dario Massa,Luca Leoni,Antoni Wadowski,Kacper Cybinski,Anshul D. S. Parmar,Carmine Autieri,Cesare Franchini,Jan Wrobel,Silvia Bonfanti,Piotr Sankowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文以Cu-Zr-Al金属玻璃为基准，系统验证了MACE基础机器学习原子间势在无序金属态上的可靠性，发现其预训练数据多样性（尤其是否包含非平衡液态与无定形态构型）是决定其精度与跨成分迁移能力的关键，而针对目标体系的微调收益有限、结晶小晶胞特化则会损害无序描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势在化学与结构空间上迁移良好，但对其在高度无序金属态（如金属玻璃）上的可靠性尚缺乏系统评估。金属玻璃传统上依赖成分特化的EAM势，而从头算分子动力学在所需尺寸与时间尺度上过于昂贵，因此亟需验证基础势能否以接近第一性原理的精度描述无序金属。

Method: 以原型Cu-Zr-Al玻璃为基准，将MACE基础模型与经典EAM势沿分子动力学淬火过程进行对比，并以密度泛函理论和实验为验证标准，先在Cu46Zr46Al8上、再拓展到三元系良好玻璃形成区。同时测试两条特化路线：一是在Lennard-Jones替代模型生成的玻璃结构上微调，二是在自动生成的小周期晶胞（ASSYST）上特化。

Result: 预训练数据库包含非平衡液态与无定形态构型（如OMAT）的模型，开箱即用即具备跨成分的精度与迁移能力，明显优于成分特化的EAM势；在目标玻璃结构上微调仅带来有限收益，而在小晶体胞上的特化会损害对无序的描述。

Conclusion: 基础势对无序金属的适用性主要由其预训练数据的多样性决定，而非体系特化的微调。这使建模重心从定制数据集生成转向提升预训练覆盖度，并需对所得到的动力学结果进行谨慎验证。

Abstract: Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials transfer well across chemical and structural space, yet their reliability for highly disordered metallic states is less established. Metallic glasses are a demanding benchmark: they have traditionally been modeled with Embedded Atom Method (EAM) potentials fitted to specific compositions, while ab initio molecular dynamics remains too expensive at the required sizes and timescales. Using the prototypical Cu-Zr-Al glass, we show that foundation potentials capture structural disorder, reproducing the structure, quenching dynamics, and elastic, mechanical and thermal properties of the amorphous state at near first-principles accuracy. We benchmark MACE foundation models against a classical EAM potential along molecular dynamics quenches, validating against density functional theory and experiment, first at Cu46Zr46Al8 and then across the good glass-forming region of the ternary. We further test two specialization routes: fine-tuning on glass structures generated with Lennard-Jones surrogate models, and on automatically generated small periodic cells (ASSYST). Models pre-trained on databases that include non-equilibrium liquid and amorphous configurations, such as OMAT, are accurate and transferable across composition out of the box, clearly outperforming the composition-specific EAM potential. Fine-tuning on target glass structures brings only modest gains, while specialization on small crystalline cells degrades the description of disorder. The readiness of foundation potentials for disordered metals is therefore set mainly by the diversity of their pre-training data rather than by system-specific fine-tuning, shifting the modeling effort from bespoke dataset generation to pre-training coverage and careful validation of the resulting dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Solvent-Mediated Disintegration of a Vitrimer Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33770)
*Manoj K Patel,Tarak K Patra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过混合分子动力学-蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了溶剂中玻璃体（vitrimer）网络随温度变化的拓扑重构与解离行为，构建温度-组成相图，揭示网络溶解临界温度随交联浓度非线性升高，且网络在宏观溶解前即开始解体，最终形成宽分布的碎片而非完全解离成单链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃体网络依赖动态可交换键实现拓扑重排，但其动态连接性在溶剂存在下（尤其随温度变化）的演化机制尚不清楚，这制约了溶剂辅助回收等应用的理解与优化。

Method: 采用混合分子动力学-蒙特卡洛（hybrid MD-MC）模拟，构建温度-组成相图，定量分析交联浓度、温度对网络重构、键重组、溶解临界温度及碎片分布的影响。

Result: 网络溶解临界温度随交联浓度非线性升高；网络解体在宏观溶解临界温度以下即已开始；高交联体系在临界温度附近发生大规模重构，形成大量更小、不连通的网络；链内动态键数量随温度升高而增加；未观察到完全解离为单链，溶液呈现宽分布的小网络碎片。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了溶剂诱导的网络解体与动态键重分布之间的复杂耦合关系，为玻璃体网络的溶解行为及其溶剂辅助回收提供分子层面见解。

Abstract: Vitrimers are dynamic polymer networks characterized by associative bond exchange, which enables topological rearrangement while preserving the overall network connectivity. However, how this dynamic connectivity evolves in the presence of a solvent, particularly as a function of temperature, remains poorly understood. Here, we employ hybrid molecular dynamics Monte Carlo simulations to elucidate the temperature-dependent restructuring and dissociation of vitrimer networks in solution. By constructing temperature composition phase diagrams, we demonstrate that the critical temperature for network dissolution increases nonlinearly with crosslink concentration. We find that the network disintegration begins well below the critical dissolution temperature, indicating that the loss of network connectivity can occur even before the onset of macroscopic dissolution. However, the network undergoes extensive restructuring around the critical temperature for a highly crosslinked system, resulting in the formation of numerous smaller, disconnected networks. Concurrently, the number of intrachain dynamic bonds increases with temperature. Interestingly, complete disintegration into individual polymer chains is not observed; instead, the solution comprises a broad distribution of smaller network fragments. These findings reveal the complex interplay between solvent-induced network disintegration and dynamic bond redistribution, providing molecular level insights into the dissolution of vitrimer networks and their potential for solvent assisted recycling.

</details>


### [100] [EGQCA Code: automated high-throughput thermodynamic modeling of solid solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34551)
*Dominik Spath,Luka Wibmer,Kieran Bozier,Ralf Meyer,Eva Kogler,Efisio Madeddu,Chris Pickard,Markus Aichhorn,Simone Di Cataldo,Luiz T. F. Eleno,Christoph Heil,Pedro N. Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个实现扩展广义准化学近似（EGQCA）的代码包，可通过有限团簇统计在任意成分和温度下对固溶体进行热力学建模，并借助通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）大幅降低计算成本，同时引入团簇选择算法以筛选关键构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全成分范围内从第一性原理建模无序体系长期存在计算挑战；传统基于DFT的团簇计算成本极高，难以支撑高通量筛选。

Method: 开发实现扩展广义准化学近似（EGQCA）的代码包，自动完成全流程并与DFT代码及通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）接口；用uMLIPs替代DFT进行团簇能量计算；并引入团簇选择算法识别最相关构型。

Result: 用uMLIPs替代DFT可将团簇计算成本降低最多五个数量级，同时保持热力学预测结果不变。

Conclusion: 这些发展使EGQCA成为固溶体热力学建模的快速、准确框架，并为无序材料的高通量筛选提供了切实可行的途径。

Abstract: Modeling disordered systems from first principles across the full composition range is a long-standing computational challenge. We present a code package implementing the extended generalized quasichemical approximation (EGQCA), which describes solid solutions through the statistics of a finite set of clusters and enables their thermodynamic modeling at arbitrary compositions and temperatures. The package automates the complete workflow and interfaces with both DFT codes and universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs). We show that replacing DFT with uMLIPs reduces the cost of the cluster calculations by up to five orders of magnitude while preserving the thermodynamic predictions. To make computationally demanding properties tractable, we additionally introduce a cluster-selection algorithm that identifies the most relevant configurations. Together, these developments establish EGQCA as a fast and accurate framework for the thermodynamic modeling of solid solutions and a practical route toward high-throughput screening of disordered materials.

</details>


### [101] [Exact subtraction of electron self-interaction from the Kohn-Sham equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33795)
*J. E. Ingall,Alister J. Page*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过约束Kohn-Sham方程中的有效势为轨道密度依赖泛函，从而满足广义库普曼条件(GKC)、消除自相互作用误差(SIE)的密度泛函方法，使LDA/GGA的基带隙平均绝对误差相比常规KS-DFT改善约1 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论(KS-DFT)中的自相互作用误差(SIE)会导致轨道能量对自身分数占据产生非物理的非线性依赖，从而产生误差。为消除自相互作用、获得正确的轨道本征能量，需要满足广义库普曼条件(GKC)，即原子轨道本征能量独立于其分数占据。

Method: 基于Levy自旋密度泛函理论的约束搜索，得到轨道密度并给出推广Hohenberg-Kohn定理的基态能量。本工作将Kohn-Sham方程的电子密度约束为依赖轨道密度，并使总能量泛函对其轨道密度变化呈线性。具体地，将轨道φ_i[n]的KS乘性有效势v_s[n]约束为泛函v_eff[n-n_i]，从而满足广义库普曼条件(GKC)。

Result: 该方法使LDA和GGA在基带隙上的平均绝对误差(MAE)相比KS-DFT改善约1 eV，满足广义库普曼条件(GKC)。

Conclusion: 不含电子自相互作用的方程为提高密度泛函理论的准确性、效率和简洁性提供了基础。

Abstract: The self-interaction error (SIE) of Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT) leads to a non-physical, non-linear dependence of an orbital's energy on its own fractional occupation [Dabo et al., Phys. Rev. B, 82:115121, 2010]. The generalized Koopmans condition (GKC) ensures an atomic orbital's eigenenergy is independent of its fractional occupation$-$ and is free of self-interaction. The constrained search of the Levy spin-density-functional theory yields orbital densities and delivers the ground-state energy to generalize the Hohenberg-Kohn theorems. In this work, the electron density of the Kohn-Sham equations is constrained to be orbital-density dependent, with a total energy functional linear with respect to variation of its orbital densities. That is, the KS multiplicative effective potential $v_{\text{s}}[{n}]$ for an orbital $\varphi_i[{n}]$ is constrained to a functional $v_{\text{eff}}[{n - n_i}]$. The result complies with the generalized Koopmans condition (GKC). This approach improves LDA and GGA mean absolute errors (MAEs) in fundamental band gaps by $\sim$ 1 eV, with respect to KS-DFT. Equations free of electron self-interaction provide the basis for improvements in the accuracy, efficiency, and simplicity of density functional theories.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Assembled Triangular Sc-rich Nanodomains: The Orign of Anomalous Alloy Scattering in ScAlN/GaN Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33868)
*Yuhao Yin,Shizhao Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ScAlN/GaN异质结中2DEG迁移率长期受限的根源,在于位错应变场驱动形成的Sc富集三角形纳米畴引发的异常合金散射;降低位错密度可显著提升迁移率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScAlN合金因具有大自发极化梯度和可实现异质界面精确晶格匹配,在GaN异质结高电子迁移率晶体管(HEMT)中潜力巨大。但尽管外延与器件工艺不断进步,ScAlN/GaN异质结构中二维电子气(2DEG)迁移率始终受限,其微观物理根源一直不明,亟需厘清。

Method: 通过揭示由位错(threading dislocations)施加的应变场起伏驱动的横向关联合金无序(laterally correlated alloy disorder)机制:位错应变场将生长前沿分割成具有不同应变状态的纳米尺度畴区,驱动Sc吸附原子各向异性扩散,形成晶体学取向的三角形Sc富集纳米畴。并通过将位错密度从2.8E10 cm^-2降低至4.4E8 cm^-2,对比纳米畴面密度及低温霍尔迁移率的变化。

Result: 位错密度降低约两个数量级(2.8E10→4.4E8 cm^-2)使Sc富集纳米畴面密度降低超过两个数量级;相应地,低温霍尔迁移率从1800 cm^2·V^-1·s^-1跃升至11995 cm^2·V^-1·s^-1。

Conclusion: 确立了位错介导的Sc富集纳米畴是异常强合金散射的主导来源,从而解决了高性能氮化物电子器件中迁移率受限的瓶颈,为提升ScAlN/GaN HEMT性能指明了方向。

Abstract: Wurtzite scandium aluminium nitride (ScAlN) alloys hold substantial potential for GaN heterostructure high-electron mobility transistors, owing to their large spontaneous polarization gradient and the ability to achieve precise lattice matching at heterointerface. Nevertheless, despite continuous advances in epitaxy and device fabrication, the two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) mobility in ScAlN/GaN heterostructures remains persistently limited, and the underlying microscopic origin remains elusive. Here, we uncover an anomalous electron scattering mechanism from laterally correlated alloy disorder that spontaneously develops within the ScAlN barrier driven by strain-field undulations imposed by threading dislocations. Dislocation strain field partitions the growth front into nanoscale domains with distinct strain states, driving anisotropic diffusion of Sc adatoms and yielding crystallographically oriented triangular Sc-rich nanodomains. Reducing the threading dislocation density from 2.8E10 to 4.4E8 cm^-2 suppresses areal density of nanodomains by more than two orders of magnitude. As a result, low-temperature Hall mobility increases from 1800 to 11995 cm^2*V^-1*s^-1. These findings establish dislocation-mediated Sc-rich nanodomains as the dominant source of anomalously strong alloy scattering, resolving the mobility bottleneck for high-performance nitride electronics.

</details>


### [103] [Cascading LO-phonon-mediated resonant energy transfer from excitons to Eu3+ for ultrahigh color purity red luminescence in europium-doped GaN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33891)
*Shizhao Fan,Yue Xu,Yuhao Yin,Shitao Dong,Shaoqiang Chen,Xueyuan Chen,Zhan Su,Datao Tu,Qian Sun,Hui Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示了GaN:Eu中持续三十年的能量转移机制之谜：Eu³⁺掺杂稳定了一个束缚激子态（BX3，3.478 eV），其经Frohlich相互作用与纵光学（LO）声子强耦合形成级联极化子（最多7个声子伴线，间距92.0±0.7 meV），其中与5个LO声子耦合的极化子恰好匹配Eu³⁺的7F0→5D3吸收能（3.019 eV），实现了共振能量转移，最终经5D3→5D0非辐射弛豫后产生622 nm高色纯度红光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效率、波长稳定且色纯度极高的红光micro-LED仍是单片III族氮化物全彩显示的关键瓶颈。GaN:Eu虽是有前景的红光方案，但其微观能量转移机制（从GaN基质到Eu³⁺离子）三十年来未被阐明，阻碍了对器件性能的理性优化。

Method: 采用等离子体辅助分子束外延（PA-MBE）生长高晶体质量GaN:Eu外延层；通过变温光致发光（PL）光谱表征声子伴线与非平衡声子发射；借助时间分辨光谱确认Eu³⁺发光位点；结合束缚激子-声子耦合（Frohlich相互作用）与极化子模型分析能量转移路径。

Result: Eu³⁺掺杂稳定了3.478 eV的束缚激子态BX3，与LO声子强耦合形成最多7个声子伴线的级联极化子（均匀能量间距92.0±0.7 meV）；耦合5个LO声子的BX3极化子能量（3.019 eV）与Eu³⁺的7F0→5D3跃迁精确匹配，实现定向共振能量转移；经5D3→5D0快速非辐射弛豫后，超灵敏5D0→7F2跃迁产生622 nm锐利红光；时间分辨光谱证实存在单一主Eu³⁺发光位点，归因于偏离轴心的EuGa-VN复合物（局域对称性降低）。

Conclusion: 本文鉴定出一条关键的能量转移通道——极化子介导的共振能量转移，为设计高效率GaN:Eu红光发射器提供了明确的框架。

Abstract: High-efficiency, wavelength-stable red micro-LEDs with ultrahigh color purity remain a critical bottleneck for monolithic III-nitride full-color displays. Europium-doped GaN (GaN:Eu) offers a compelling solution, yet the microscopic energy transfer mechanism from the GaN host to Eu3+ ions has remained unresolved for three decades, impeding rational performance optimization. Here we report polaron-mediated resonant energy transfer in high-crystalline-quality GaN:Eu epilayers grown by plasma-assisted molecular beam epitaxy. Eu3+ doping stabilizes a distinct bound exciton (BX3) state at 3.478 eV, which couples strongly with longitudinal optical (LO) phonons via the Frohlich interaction to form cascading polarons with up to seven phonon replicas at a uniform energy spacing of 92.0+/-0.7 meV. The BX3 polaron coupled with 5LO phonons matches precisely the 7F0-->5D3 intra-4f transition energy of Eu3+ at 3.019 eV. Temperature-dependent photoluminescence reveals non-equilibrium phonon emission, evidencing directional resonant energy transfer. After rapid nonradiative relaxation from the 5D3 excited state to the 5D0 metastable state, the hypersensitive 5D0-->7F2 transition yields sharp 622 nm red emission. Time-resolved spectroscopy confirms a single dominant Eu3+ luminescent site with reduced local symmetry, attributed to off-axis EuGa-VN complexes. This work identifies a key energy transfer channel and provides a clear framework for designing high-efficiency GaN:Eu red emitter.

</details>


### [104] [Wannier-Based Low-Energy Electronic Structure Analysis of $λ$-(STF)$_2$GaCl$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34000)
*Arifa D. Agustin,Aulia A. Firdaus,Fathan M. Santana,Shuhei Fukuoka,Atsushi Kawamoto,Muhammad R. Ramadhan,Isao Watanabe,Dita P. Sari,Yasuyuki Ishii*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用DFT和MLWFs研究了λ-(BETS)₂GaCl₄与λ-(STF)₂GaCl₄的低能电子结构，发现STF相对BETS具有更窄的前沿能带和显著减小的t_d1转移积分（51→6.5 meV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究BETS与STF两种分子在λ型盐中低能电子结构的差异，明确后者对前沿能带宽度和层内电子连接模式的调控效应。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）和最大局域化Wannier函数（MLWFs）方法；对λ-(STF)₂GaCl₄比较了DFT优选与XRD直接精修的结构模型在固定晶格弛豫前后的差异；对每个选定的未弛豫结构构造两个MLWFs以表示上（前沿）能带，并计算Wannier转移积分。

Result: 两种初始结构模型近费米能级电子结构在弛豫后趋于相似；相对于BETS，弛豫后的STF前沿能带宽度更窄但仍保持显著的二聚化能标；BETS的MLWFs总展宽为36.708 Å²，STF为37.578 Å²；Wannier转移积分显示t_d1由BETS的51 meV剧减至STF的6.5 meV，表明低能电子连通性被选择性改变。

Conclusion: STF取代BETS后，通过减小前沿带宽和强烈压低t_d1转移积分，实现了对低能电子连接方式的选择性调控，为理解及设计相关分子导体提供了依据。

Abstract: The low-energy electronic structures of $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$ and $λ$-(STF)$_2$GaCl$_4$ were investigated using density functional theory (DFT) and maximally localized Wannier functions (MLWFs). For $λ$-(STF)$_2$GaCl$_4$, DFT-selected and directly XRD-refined structural models were compared before and after fixed-lattice relaxation. Their initially different near-Fermi-level electronic structures become similar after relaxation. Relative to BETS, relaxed STF has a narrower frontier bandwidth while retaining a substantial dimerization energy scale. Two MLWFs were constructed for each selected unrelaxed structure to represent the upper frontier bands, with total spreads of 36.708 Å$^2$ for BETS and 37.578 Å$^2$ for STF. The Wannier-derived transfer integrals show a pronounced reduction of $t_{d1}$ from 51 meV in BETS to 6.5 meV in STF, indicating selective modification of the low-energy electronic connectivity.

</details>


### [105] [Phono3GPU: GPU-Accelerated Linear Tetrahedron Integration for Phonon Thermal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33912)
*Jinhyeong Jo,Sungwoo Kang,Byungju Lee,Jung-Hoon Lee,Seugchul Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Phono3GPU，一种将三声子散射与线性四面体积分重新设计为大规模并行 GPU 执行的计算工具，显著降低了基于四面体方法的热导率计算成本，并实现了缺陷硅超胞的原子分辨散射分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热管理和能源技术对高/低热导率材料需求日益增长；机器学习原子间势加速了非谐力常数计算，使瓶颈转向大规模数据集上的声子输运计算。现有 GPU 求解器依赖展宽（smearing）参数（需按材料调整），而 phono3py 使用的线性四面体法虽避免该参数，但对复杂结构计算代价高昂。

Method: 作者提出 Phono3GPU，将三声子散射过程与线性四面体积分重新表述，以适配大规模并行 GPU 执行，从而在保持四面体法无展宽参数优势的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Result: 在基准晶体上，Phono3GPU 相比 CPU 实现将总计算加速最高达 41 倍（复杂单胞增益更大），相比近期 phono3py 的 GPU 插件加速 2 至 6 倍；并展示了缺陷硅超胞中的原子分辨散射分析应用。

Conclusion: Phono3GPU 降低了基于四面体的热导率计算成本，并支持对结构相关声子输运的精细研究，有望推动大规模材料数据集的热输运计算与深入分析。

Abstract: Materials with high or low thermal conductivity are increasingly important for thermal management and energy technologies. Machine-learned interatomic potentials have accelerated anharmonic force-constant calculations, shifting the bottleneck toward phonon transport calculations for large materials datasets. Existing GPU solvers often rely on smearing, whose width can require material-dependent choices, while the linear tetrahedron method used in phono3py avoids this parameter but becomes costly for complex structures. Here we introduce Phono3GPU, which reformulates three-phonon scattering and linear tetrahedron integration for massively parallel GPU execution. Across benchmark crystals, Phono3GPU accelerates total calculations by up to 41 times relative to a CPU implementation, with larger gains for complex unit cells, and by 2 to 6 times relative to a recent GPU plug-in for phono3py. We further demonstrate atom-resolved scattering analysis in defective silicon supercells as an application enabled by the reduced computational cost. Phono3GPU thus lowers the cost of tetrahedron-based thermal-conductivity calculations and of detailed investigations of structure-dependent phonon transport.

</details>


### [106] [PerovAgent: Autonomous Ligand Design for Halide Perovskite Nanocrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33913)
*Seungjun Cha,Yoo Jae Jeong,Amanda Conde Del Moral,Juan-Pablo Correa-Baena,Seth R. Marder,David S. Ginger,Victor Fung,Guoxiang Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出PerovAgent，一个结合大语言模型科学推理与原子模拟、机器学习势函数和统计验证的自主多智能体框架，用于在CsPbBr3表面设计配体并从中提炼可验证的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计高性能卤化物钙钛矿纳米晶的表面封端配体需要在庞大的分子空间中搜索，同时还需发现支配配体-表面相互作用的可泛化设计规则；单纯优化难以转化为可理解、可检验的科学知识。

Method: PerovAgent将LLM驱动的科学推理与计算工具链结合：原子模型构建、机器学习原子间势函数评估及统计验证。在CsPbBr3表面进行十轮独立campaign，自适应探索并精炼多样化配体化学，并以结合能为优化目标；同时将反复出现的化学观察转化为显式设计原则，进行统计评估与混杂因素审计（confound audit），并用密度泛函理论进一步验证。

Result: 框架持续提升结合能目标；共提出35条设计原则，其中19条获得前瞻性支持，仅7条通过混杂因素审计，说明需要系统验证LLM生成的假设。一个反复出现的炔基-膦酸酯原则经DFT复现了预测的能量偏好。框架还可扩展至两性离子、多齿和可聚合配体。

Conclusion: PerovAgent证明自主发现框架不仅能搜索分子空间并优化标量目标，还能将搜索轨迹转化为可系统检验的设计原则，为卤化物钙钛矿纳米晶配体设计及更广泛配体化学探索开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Designing surface-capping ligands for high-performance halide perovskite nanocrystals requires navigating a vast molecular space while discovering design rules that govern ligand-surface interactions. Here, we introduce PerovAgent, an autonomous multi-agent framework that combines large language model (LLM)-driven scientific reasoning with computational tools for atomistic model construction, machine-learned interatomic potential evaluation, and statistical validation. Across ten independent campaigns on CsPbBr3 surfaces, PerovAgent adaptively explored and refined diverse ligand chemistries while progressively improving the binding-energy objective. Beyond optimization, the framework converts recurring chemical observations into explicit design principles that can be statistically evaluated. Of 35 formulated principles, 19 were prospectively supported, of which seven also passed the confound audit, highlighting the need to systematically validate plausible LLM-generated hypotheses during autonomous discovery. A recurrent alkynyl-phosphonate principle was further examined using density functional theory, which reproduced the predicted energetic preference. These results demonstrate an autonomous discovery framework that not only searches molecular space and optimizes the scalar objective but also transforms its search trajectory into systematically testable design principles. The framework can also be extended to more complex ligands, including zwitterionic, multidentate, and polymerizable ligands, to explore a wider range of ligand-surface interactions and uncover new design principles for halide perovskite nanocrystals.

</details>


### [107] [Revisiting the Schottky-Mott Relation for Metal/ Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Interfaces: The Role of Interface Dipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33929)
*Chih-Piao Chuu,Shu-Jui Chang,Yu-Miin Sheu,Zih-Syuan Huang,Wei-Tung Liu,Po-Wei Chen,Sui-An Chou,Yu-Tung Lin,Chih-I Wu,Wen-Hao Chang,Chun-Liang Lin,H. -S. Philip Wong,Iuliana P. Radu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算与UPS/STS实验，重新审视二维半导体中费米能级钉扎的本质，确立金属有效功函数作为验证肖特基-莫特关系的关键指标，并揭示FLP由长程偶极相互作用与扩展短程Lennard-Jones势共同主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高接触电阻主要源于费米能级钉扎（FLP），是过渡金属硫族化合物（TMDs）二维场效应晶体管接触工程的关键瓶颈，因此需要重新审视二维半导体中FLP的内在本质，尤其是在材料厚度与界面厚度相当的区域。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算与实验验证相结合的方法，通过紫外光电子能谱（UPS）和扫描隧道谱（STS）进行实验表征，确立金属有效功函数（eWF）作为验证肖特基-莫特关系的核心指标，并分析长程偶极相互作用与扩展短程Lennard-Jones势对FLP的贡献。

Result: 研究发现FLP本质上由长程偶极相互作用和扩展短程Lennard-Jones势共同控制；金属有效功函数被确立为验证肖特基-莫特关系的确定性指标（figure of merit）。

Conclusion: 该框架结合线性肖特基-莫特关系，可提高接触工程中的预测能力，为TMDs二维场效应晶体管的接触优化提供理论依据。

Abstract: High contact resistance, predominantly stemming from Fermi-level pinning (FLP), becomes a critical bottleneck in the contact engineering of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) based two-dimensional field-effect transistors (2DFETs). Our study re-examines the inherent nature of FLP in 2D semiconductors, particularly focusing on regimes where the material thickness is commensurate with the interface thickness. Through the synergistic application of first-principles calculations and experimental validation via ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy (UPS) and scanning tunneling spectroscopy (STS), we rigorously establish the metal effective work function (eWF) as the definitive figure of merit for substantiating the Schottky-Mott relation. Our findings indicate that FLP is intrinsically governed by both long-range dipole interactions and extended short-range Lennard-Jones potentials. This framework, in conjunction with the linear Schottky-Mott relation, improves predictive capabilities in contact engineering.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Geometric Origin of Néel-Order-Driven Magneto-Optical Response in MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34076)
*Suman Rooj,M. S. Mrudul,Daegeun Jo,Peter M. Oppeneer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在交变磁体 MnTe 中证明，Néel 序的对称性通过量子几何（量子度规与 Berry 曲率）的独立贡献直接映射到磁光响应上：量子度规产生二重光学各向异性（E₂g 双态，对应 Voigt 信号），Berry 曲率产生三重霍尔响应（A₂g 不变量），且后者并非源于净磁化，而是直接来自补偿的 Néel 序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁光响应是探测磁序的直接手段，但分辨补偿型反铁磁序仍然困难。作者希望找到将 Néel 序（尤其是交变磁序）的对称性直接反映到磁光响应中的物理机制与判据。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算与朗道对称性框架，分析 MnTe 中量子度规与 Berry 曲率对磁光响应的贡献，并将其按 Néel 序不变量的对称性（E₂g 与 A₂g）进行分类；同时通过约束面外弱铁磁矩为零，检验霍尔响应与净磁化的关系。

Result: 量子度规给出二重光学各向异性，其对角与对称非对角分量构成与二次 Néel 序不变量关联的 E₂g 双态，提供表征 Néel 矢量取向的磁光 Voigt 信号；Berry 曲率给出由三次 A₂g Néel 序不变量支配的互补三重霍尔响应。霍尔电导率与面外弱铁磁矩具有相同对称性和角度依赖，但将后者设为零后光学霍尔响应基本不变，说明霍尔响应直接源于补偿 Néel 序而非净磁化。

Conclusion: 量子几何构成 Néel 序与磁光响应之间的直接联系，在交变磁体 MnTe 中揭示了 Néel 序的二重与三重光学特征。

Abstract: Magneto-optical (MO) responses provide a direct probe of magnetic order, yet resolving compensated antiferromagnetic order remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate in altermagnetic MnTe that the symmetry of the Néel order is imprinted on the MO response through distinct quantum-geometric contributions. Combining first-principles calculations with a Landau symmetry framework, we demonstrate that the quantum metric gives rise to a twofold optical anisotropy, with its diagonal and symmetric off-diagonal components forming an $E_{2g}$ doublet associated with the quadratic Néel-order invariants, providing a characteristic MO Voigt signature of the Néel-vector orientation. In contrast, the Berry curvature generates a complementary threefold Hall response governed by the cubic $A_{2g}$ Néel-order invariant. Although the Hall conductivity and the out-of-plane weak-ferromagnetic moment share the same symmetry and angular dependence, constraining the latter to zero leaves the optical Hall response essentially unchanged demonstrating that the Hall response originates directly from the compensated Néel order rather than the net magnetization. Our results establish quantum geometry as a direct link between Néel order and MO response, revealing distinct twofold and threefold optical signatures of Néel order in altermagnetic MnTe.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Metric Connects Bond Delocalization to Phonon Softening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34118)
*Tianqi Deng,Tiejun Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 作者通过动量空间的量子度规（quantum metric）建立了电子离域与声子软化之间的定量联系：量子几何对声子软化的贡献正比于积分量子度规，并通过对百余种化合物的第一性原理计算验证了量子度规越大、声子越软的普遍规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离域键合电子长期以来被认为与软键和低频率声子相关，但电子离域与声子软化之间缺乏定量的关联。作者旨在借助量子几何（量子度规）这一刻画占据电子态内禀离域程度的物理量，建立二者之间的定量关系。

Method: 作者将声子软化中的量子几何贡献与动量空间积分量子度规成正比地联系起来，并通过第一性原理计算对一百余种化合物进行验证；同时利用局域化的 Wannier 轨道揭示与离域相关的键合环境，并结合典型案例（铁电材料 GeTe、LiBeSb、BaTiO₃ 以及 PbSe）分析量子度规、键合轨道局域化与声子软化之间的关联。在 PbSe 中还考察了压力诱导能带反转的影响。

Result: 计算表明，量子度规越大通常伴随声子越软。在铁电材料 GeTe、LiBeSb 和 BaTiO₃ 中，极化畸变使键合轨道局域化、量子度规减小，并稳定了非极性结构的软模；在 PbSe 中，压力诱导的能带反转伴随量子度规增强和声子软化，且不改变晶体对称性与键合。

Conclusion: 上述结果确立了不同键合环境下键离域与声子软化的统一量子几何解释：量子度规作为电子离域的定量度量，能够普遍地解释和预测声子软化行为。

Abstract: Delocalized bonding electrons have long been associated with soft bonds and low phonon frequencies. However, a quantitative connection between electron delocalization and phonon softening remains elusive. We identify such a connection through the momentum-space quantum metric, which measures the intrinsic delocalization of the occupied electronic states. We show that a quantum geometric contribution to phonon softening is proportional to the integrated quantum metric. First-principles calculations of more than a hundred compounds demonstrate that greater quantum metric generally accompanies softer phonons. Localized Wannier orbitals reveal the bonding environments associated with this delocalization. In ferroelectric GeTe, LiBeSb, and BaTiO$_3$, polar distortion localizes the bonding orbitals, reduces quantum metric, and stabilizes the soft mode of the nonpolar structure. In PbSe, pressure-induced band inversion is accompanied by an enhanced quantum metric and phonon softening without change in crystal symmetry and bonding. These results establish a common quantum geometric interpretation of bond delocalization and phonon softening across distinct bonding environments.

</details>


### [110] [Magnetization, susceptibility, and entanglement entropy in antiferromagnetic star-shaped homometallic tetranuclear and pentanuclear Cu(II) complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34281)
*Gor Mirzoyan,Vladimir V. Papoyan,Andrea Trombettoni,Nikita Titov,Lev Ananikyan,Nerses Ananikian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了星形四核（Cu₄）与五核（Cu₅）反铁磁 Cu(II) 团簇的磁化强度、磁化率及量子纠缠熵，揭示团簇尺寸、几何结构与各向异性对热力学和纠缠性质的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受实验中已实现的分子自旋体系启发，作者希望从理论上刻画星形多核 Cu(II) 团簇的磁学与纠缠行为，并将理论解析计算与实验磁化率数据进行对比验证。

Method: 基于反铁磁 Heisenberg 自旋哈密顿量，并引入 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用，对星形四核（Cu₄）和五核（Cu₅）团簇进行理论分析，计算磁化强度、磁化率随温度的变化，并分析低温下磁化强度跳跃、磁化率峰与纠缠熵平台间的关系。

Result: 理论解析的磁化率-温度曲线与实验数据吻合较好；低温下磁化强度跳跃与磁化率峰存在对应关系，且外磁场下纠缠熵呈现平台结构。团簇尺寸、几何构型及相互作用各向异性对热力学性质和纠缠性质均起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 星形多核 Cu(II) 团簇的磁学与纠缠特性强烈依赖于团簇规模、几何及各向异性；通过引入 DM 相互作用的 Heisenberg 模型可有效描述实验观察到的磁化率行为及低温场下的量子纠缠特征。

Abstract: We present a theoretical investigation of the magnetization, magnetic susceptibility, and quantum entanglement entropy in antiferromagnetic star-shaped tetranuclear ($Cu_4$) and pentanuclear ($Cu_5$) Cu(II) clusters, motivated by experimentally realized molecular spin systems. In both cases, the study presents a discussion of the comparison between experimental data and theoretical analytical calculations of magnetic susceptibility as a function of temperature. The correlation between magnetization jumps and magnetic susceptibility peaks, along with the presence of entanglement entropy plateaus as a function of external magnetic fields, is analyzed in the low-temperature regime. Overall, the results demonstrate that the cluster size, geometry, and interaction anisotropy play a crucial role in shaping both the thermodynamic and the entanglement properties. In addition, the study is based on antiferromagnetic Heisenberg spin Hamiltonians, with the inclusion of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction.

</details>


### [111] [Anisotropic magnetic-field modulation of hysteretic sheet resistance in few-layer ferroelectric WTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34259)
*Arpan Manna,Soumik Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了少层WTe2（极性半金属）中铁电性的磁输运特征，发现垂直磁场显著增强其铁电回滞，而面内磁场几乎无响应，揭示了各向异性磁输运与铁电序之间的密切关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少层WTe2的铁电性源于层间滑移，可通过栅极依赖的电阻回滞探测，但磁场在该输运特征中的作用此前尚未被系统探究，因此需要阐明磁场如何影响这一铁电输运信号。

Method: 作者在剥离的少层WTe2器件中施加垂直与面内磁场，对比两种磁场方向对栅极依赖电阻回滞的影响；进一步分析回滞的单个分支，提取两个铁电态各自的磁输运响应，并考察增强效应的磁场幂律依赖关系及其随温度的演化。

Result: 垂直磁场产生显著的回滞增强，而面内磁场响应可忽略；两个铁电态在磁场下呈现不同的磁输运响应，导致其电阻分离增大。回滞增强表现出稳健的幂律磁场依赖，且其温度演化与磁电阻行为高度相似。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了各向异性磁输运与铁电回滞之间的紧密关联，凸显了二维极性半金属中磁响应与铁性序之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Ferroelectricity in few-layer WTe2, a polar semimetal, originates from interlayer sliding and can be probed through gate-dependent resistance hysteresis. Yet the role of magnetic fields in this transport signature remains largely unexplored. Here, we examine magnetic-field-induced changes in the hysteresis in exfoliated few-layer WTe2. Applying an out-of-plane magnetic field produces a pronounced hysteresis enhancement, while an in-plane field yields a negligible response. Analysis of the individual hysteresis branches reveals distinct magnetotransport responses of the two ferroelectric states, which increase their resistance separation under magnetic field. The enhancement exhibits a robust power-law field dependence and a temperature evolution closely resembling that of magnetoresistance. Our results reveal a close association between anisotropic magnetotransport and ferroelectric hysteresis, underscoring the interplay between magnetic response and ferroic order in a two-dimensional polar semimetal.

</details>


### [112] [Electronic topological modes in hexagonal NbSi$_{2}$ with a double helix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34356)
*Issam Mahraj,Andrzej Ptok*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究六角形手性NbSi₂的电子性质，发现其拓扑边缘态源于结构手性而非自旋轨道耦合，并确认了携带Chern数±2的Weyl点、扩展费米弧及定域在手性Si链上的表面态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性体系可承载非平庸拓扑态（如特征边缘模和费米弧），但如何将拓扑边缘态与结构手性明确关联、以及弱自旋轨道耦合下拓扑态的起源机制尚需厘清。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对六角形手性NbSi₂的体材料与slab几何结构进行分析；通过Berry曲率分析确认Weyl点及其Chern数；并利用空间分辨与位点依赖的态密度（DOS）研究表面态的实空间局域性。

Result: 发现自旋轨道耦合较弱；拓扑边缘态源于结构手性而非SOC；确认存在Weyl点，部分携带Chern数±2；在高对称点附近观察到具有扩展费米弧的手性边缘模；表面态可唯一归属于手性Si链。

Conclusion: 六角形NbSi₂中，结构手性是驱动拓扑边缘态和费米弧出现的关键因素，而非自旋轨道耦合，且这些表面态定域在手性Si链上。

Abstract: Chiral systems can host nontrivial topological states, including characteristic edge modes and Fermi arcs. Here, we investigate the electronic properties of hexagonal NbSi$_2$ with chiral symmetry using first-principles calculations for both bulk and slab geometries. We find that spin--orbit coupling (SOC) is relatively weak. Notably, the topological edge state originates from structural chirality rather than the SOC. From the Berry curvature analysis, we confirm the existence of Weyl points, some of which carry a Chern number of $\pm 2$. Furthermore, we demonstrate the existence of chiral edge modes with extended Fermi arcs around high-symmetry points. Finally, we investigate the real-space localization using spatially resolved and site-dependent density of states (DOS). We demonstrate that the surface states can be exclusively associated with the chiral Si chains.

</details>


### [113] [LetzElPhC: an electron-phonon code for efficient exciton-phonon workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34500)
*Muralidhar Nalabothula,Ludger Wirtz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: LetzElPhC 是一款开源软件包，可从 Quantum ESPRESSO 的密度泛函微扰计算结果出发计算电子-声子矩阵元，并与 Yambo 接口、利用晶体对称性，实现全布里渊区上严格规范一致的激子-声子矩阵元计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子耦合的从头算模拟需要计算电子-声子及激子-声子矩阵元，但直接计算往往面临计算成本高、内存需求大，以及一电子布洛赫态与 Bethe-Salpeter 激子波函数之间相位（规范）不一致带来的相位模糊问题，阻碍了大规模、高精度的声子辅助光谱模拟。

Method: LetzElPhC 直接从 Quantum ESPRESSO 的密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）输出计算电子-声子矩阵元；通过与 Yambo 接口并利用晶体对称性，在全布里渊区强制执行一电子布洛赫态与 Bethe-Salpeter 激子波函数之间的严格规范一致性，从而避免相位模糊并降低成本与内存。为支持稠密动量网格的大规模计算，该软件还引入动力学矩阵和微扰 Kohn-Sham 势的傅里叶插值，考虑二维和三维材料中的长程偶极与四极相互作用，并配备多级并行化和高效并行 I/O。

Result: 该软件包可无缝配合 Yambo 实现声子辅助光学谱的从头算模拟，并成功应用于单层 WSe2 的共振拉曼散射和立方 AgBr 的声子辅助发光，验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: LetzElPhC 通过对称性保持的规范一致方案与高效并行插值策略，显著降低了激子-声子矩阵元计算的成本与内存需求，并可作为 Yambo 的有力补充，支撑二维/三维材料中各类声子辅助光学谱学的第一性原理模拟。

Abstract: We present \texttt{LetzElPhC}, an open-source software package that computes electron-phonon matrix elements from the output of density functional perturbation theory calculations performed with the \texttt{Quantum ESPRESSO} code. By interfacing directly with the \texttt{Yambo} code and exploiting crystal symmetries, \texttt{LetzElPhC} enables a scalable workflow for evaluating exciton-phonon matrix elements while enforcing strict gauge consistency between one-electron Bloch states and Bethe-Salpeter excitonic wave functions across the full Brillouin zone. This symmetry-preserving approach avoids phase ambiguities and significantly reduces both computational costs and memory requirements. To support large-scale simulations on dense momentum grids, the package incorporates Fourier interpolation of dynamical matrices and perturbed Kohn-Sham potentials, accounting for long-range dipole and quadrupole interactions in both two- and three-dimensional materials, combined with multi-level parallelization and efficient parallel I/O. Together, these capabilities seamlessly complement the \texttt{Yambo} code to enable first-principles simulations of phonon-assisted optical spectroscopies, which we demonstrate through applications to resonant Raman scattering in monolayer $\text{WSe}_2$ and phonon-assisted luminescence in cubic AgBr.

</details>


### [114] [AFM metrology for 3D high aspect ratio semiconductor structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34507)
*Hokyun Chin,Shihwei Yu,Lucas Rencker,Omid Tajalizadehkhoob,Khalid El Maghawry Elsayed,Po-Cheng Wu,Duygu Akbulut,Niranjan Saikumar,Hamed Sadeghian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用原子力显微镜（AFM）对高深宽比（HAR）环绕栅极场效应晶体管（GAAFET）结构进行测量，清晰分辨出纳米片（NS）区域及纳米片之间隔离区中线条与间隔的独立轮廓，并对关键尺寸进行了全面统计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着晶体管架构持续创新和沉积、光刻技术的进步，集成电路结构复杂度不断提升，对制造过程控制提出更严格要求，亟需快速可靠的计量方案来表征高深宽比GAAFET型结构的关键参数。

Method: 采用原子力显微镜（AFM）作为计量手段，对高深宽比环绕栅极场效应晶体管（GAAFET）型结构进行高分辨率测量，在纳米片（NS）区域和纳米片之间的隔离区分别分辨线条与间隔的独立轮廓，并对关键尺寸（CD）开展稳健的统计分析。

Result: 成功清晰分辨出纳米片区域及纳米片间隔离区中线条与间隔的独立轮廓，并获得了其关键尺寸的全面统计评估结果，验证了AFM在高深宽比GAAFET结构计量中的可靠性与有效性。

Conclusion: 原子力显微镜能够为高深宽比GAAFET型结构提供快速、可靠的计量解决方案，可有效支撑先进晶体管制造中的过程控制。

Abstract: Transistors have long been the foundation of the semiconductor industry, with continuous architectural innovations enabling the scaling of integrated circuits while maintaining high performance. Advancements in deposition and lithographic techniques have further expanded design possibilities, while increasing structural complexity demands stricter process control. Atomic force microscopy (AFM) offers a rapid and reliable metrology solution, providing high-resolution measurements and robust statistical analysis of critical parameters. In this work, we present clearly resolved individual profiles of lines and spaces in high aspect ratio (HAR) gate all around FET (GAAFET)-type structures on both the nanosheet (NS) region and in the isolation region between the nanosheets and we present a comprehensive statistical evaluation of their critical dimensions.

</details>


### [115] [Magnetic Reconfiguration Controls Spin Splitting and Spin-Orbit-Coupled Responses in Compensated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34513)
*Yongqian Zhu,Lu Liu,Xiaobing Chen,Tong Zou,Yuntian Liu,Zehao Ji,Qihang Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明磁重构（仅反转局域磁矩而不改变晶格结构）可在自旋简并的反铁磁体、交变磁体和补偿型亚铁磁体中，以极小的磁能代价引发自旋劈裂与反常霍尔响应的质变，为补偿磁体的电子与输运调控提供了对称性指导的新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿磁体是自旋依赖电子与输运现象的理想平台，但如何在几乎不改变能量和晶体结构的前提下，大幅改造其自旋劈裂与自旋轨道耦合响应，仍缺乏系统理解；作者希望揭示磁构型切换能带来多大的物理性质变化。

Method: 结合紧束缚模型、取向自旋空间群分析和第一性原理计算，系统比较反铁磁体（AFM）、交变磁体（AM）和补偿型亚铁磁体（cFIM）在不同磁构型下的自旋劈裂机制与反常霍尔响应；并在MAGNDATA数据库筛选12个cFIM候选，以Mn2Mo3O8为例验证，进一步从共线AFM设计出MnGeO3和Co2Mo3O8作为cFIM。

Result: 发现近简并能量的磁构型可呈现定性不同的自旋劈裂能带和反常霍尔响应。将AM构型转变为cFIM态会激活交错的在位势（staggered onsite-potential）对自旋劈裂的贡献，与AM的各向异性跃迁机制互补并消除对称性强制的简并；cFIM对称性允许一阶自旋轨道耦合的反常霍尔响应，优于对应AM态的高阶或取向受限响应。实验中在Mn2Mo3O8中验证了该机制，并通过MnGeO3、Co2Mo3O8展示其普适性。

Conclusion: 磁重构是一种强大的对称性引导策略，能够以微小磁能成本显著重塑补偿磁体的电子结构与输运响应，为调控自旋劈裂和反常霍尔效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Compensated magnets provide a versatile platform for spin-dependent electronic and transport phenomena. Here, we show that magnetic reconfiguration, i.e., selective reversal of local moments without changing the underlying crystal structure, can profoundly transform the nonrelativistic spin splitting and spin-orbit-coupled responses of spin-degenerate antiferromagnets (AFMs), altermagnets (AMs), and compensated ferrimagnets (cFIMs). Strikingly, magnetic configurations with nearly degenerate energies can exhibit qualitatively different spin-split band structures and anomalous Hall responses, allowing a small magnetic-energy scale to produce large changes in physical properties. Combining tight-binding models, oriented spin space group analysis, and first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that converting an AM configuration into cFIM states activates staggered onsite-potential contributions to spin splitting, complementing the anisotropic-hopping mechanism of AMs and removing symmetry-enforced degeneracies. Moreover, cFIM symmetry permits an anomalous Hall response at first order in spin-orbit coupling, in contrast to the higher-order or orientation-constrained responses of corresponding AM states. We demonstrate this regime in Mn2Mo3O8, one of 12 cFIM candidates identified in the MAGNDATA database, and further demonstrate its generality by designing cFIMs from collinear AFMs, exemplified by MnGeO3 and Co2Mo3O8. Our results establish magnetic reconfiguration as a powerful symmetry-guided strategy for dramatically reshaping electronic and transport responses in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [116] [High-Throughput Orientation Extraction via Symmetry-Adapted Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34541)
*Caden Myers,Yucong Chen,Nathan Chalus,Morten R. Eskildsen,Tess Smidt,Simon J. L. Billinge*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性、无需训练的对称性自适应滤波器（SAF）算法，通过在目标对称中心旋转编码了已知旋转对称性和峰宽的滤波器以最大化其与图像的内积，从而确定物体取向，并在2万余张小角中子散射图像和原子分辨实空间图像上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种快速、可复现且无需训练的方法来确定具有已知旋转对称性物体在图像中的取向，以替代依赖训练或随机性的传统算法。

Method: SAF算法：构造一个编码已知旋转对称性与近似峰宽的滤波器，将其中心置于目标物体的对称中心，并按感兴趣区域进行掩模处理，随后旋转该滤波器使其与图像的内积最大化，从而确定物体取向。

Result: 该算法在逾2万张小角度中子散射图像（扭矩驱动的斯格明子晶格）上得到验证，并在原子分辨实空间图像中成功映射局部原子取向，用于识别晶粒与晶界及量化晶格弛豫。

Conclusion: SAF算法具有确定性、无需训练、快速且可复现的优点，能够有效确定具有已知旋转对称性物体的取向，并在多种成像技术（中子散射与原子分辨成像）中展现广泛的适用性。

Abstract: An algorithm for finding the orientation of objects in an image that have a known rotational symmetry, the symmetry-adapted filter (SAF) algorithm, is presented. It is deterministic and training-free, which makes it fast and reproducible. A filter that encodes the known rotational symmetry and approximate peak width is centered on the symmetry center of the object of interest, masked to the region of interest, and rotated to maximize its inner product with the image. The approach is demonstrated on more than 20,000 small-angle neutron scattering images of a torque-driven skyrmion lattice. It is also demonstrated on atomically resolved real-space images, where mapping local atomic orientations enabled the identification of grains and grain boundaries and the quantification of lattice relaxation.

</details>


### [117] [Origin of the anomalous shape of the $γ$-loop in paramagnetic Fe1-xCrx alloys from coupled Atomistic Spin Dynamics and ab initio Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34565)
*Joakim Stenborg,Davide Gambino,Björn Alling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用原子自旋动力学与从头算分子动力学耦合及热力学积分，计算了Fe-Cr合金（Cr含量0–15 at.%）fcc与bcc相在800–1800 K的吉布斯自由能差，成功复现了实验上反常的γ-环路及其成分依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-Cr合金富铁区fcc稳定区（γ-环路）形状反常：低温下少量Cr先稳定fcc，但超过8 at.%后转而稳定bcc；高温下Cr恒稳定bcc。该行为源于磁、振动与构型自由能贡献间的微妙平衡，用第一性原理方法难以准确捕捉，亟需一种能同时、等权重处理这些效应的计算方法。

Method: 采用耦合原子自旋动力学（ASD）与从头算分子动力学（AIMD），辅以热力学积分方法，计算Fe1-xCrx（x=0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15）在800–1800 K范围内fcc与bcc之间的吉布斯自由能差；并结合磁有序态与无序态的静态计算分析磁性、振动及化学短程聚集的贡献。

Result: 计算以近定量精度（γ-δ转变）复现了实验γ-环路及其成分依赖；α-γ边界（含其反常形状）被定性复现，但转变温度被低估约200 K。结果表明：磁有序态中Cr增加起初降低bcc相对fcc的能优势，而磁无序态中Cr在所有成分下均持续稳定bcc；振动效应对相稳定有贡献；较高Cr含量（x≥0.15）下原子的化学短程聚集可解释γ-环路自下方闭合。顺磁态中Cr表现出随局域磁环境变化的有限磁矩。

Conclusion: 反常γ-环路由磁能量、磁无序、晶格振动与化学聚集的协同作用决定，唯有将这些效应放在同等地位统一处理，方能第一性原理地捕捉相稳定性。

Abstract: The region of fcc stability in Fe-rich Fe-Cr alloys, the $γ$-loop, has an anomalous shape: at low temperatures, Cr addition initially stabilizes fcc, but above 8 at.% Cr it stabilizes bcc. At high temperatures, Cr always stabilizes bcc. This behavior reflects a delicate balance between magnetic, vibrational and configurational free-energy contributions that is challenging to capture from first principles. We investigate phase stability in Fe1-xCrx (x = 0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15) using coupled atomistic spin dynamics and ab initio molecular dynamics with thermodynamic integration. Gibbs free energy differences between fcc and bcc are calculated from 800 to 1800 K. Our calculations reproduce the experimental $γ$-loop and its compositional dependence, with near-quantitative accuracy for the $γ$-$δ$ transition. The $α$-$γ$ boundary, including its anomalous shape, is reproduced qualitatively, although transition temperatures are underestimated by approximately 200 K. Static calculations of magnetically ordered and disordered states provide an explanation: in ordered states, increasing Cr initially reduces the energetic preference for bcc over fcc, whereas in disordered states, Cr progressively stabilizes bcc at all compositions. Vibrational effects contribute to phase stability, while atomic short-range clustering at higher Cr content ($x \geq 0.15$) may explain the observed closing of the $γ$-loop from below. The simulations reveal finite Cr moments in the paramagnetic state, whose magnitudes depend on their local magnetic environment. The results demonstrate that the anomalous $γ$-loop arises from the interplay of magnetic energetics, magnetic disorder, lattice vibrations and chemical clustering, which must be treated on equal footing to capture phase stability from first principle

</details>


### [118] [Frozen Phonons on Dense $\mathbf{q}$-Grids Using Set Covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34638)
*Sebastian Riis Thomsen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定最小超胞集合的算法，使任意 q 点网格上的每个点都与至少一个超胞相容，从而在保留非对角超胞法减小超胞尺寸优势的同时，消除为每个 q 点单独构建超胞的冗余，大幅降低冻结声子法计算声子动力学矩阵的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结声子法通过 Hellmann-Feynman 力的有限差分求动力学矩阵，但通常需要极大的超胞；非对角超胞法通过寻找与给定 q 点相容的最小超胞来减小尺寸，却要为网格上每个 q 点构建新超胞，而单个非对角超胞往往同时与多个 q 点相容，存在冗余。本文旨在消除这种冗余。

Method: 提出一种确定最小超胞集合的方法，使 q 网格上每一个 q 点都与集合中至少一个超胞相容；给出求解该问题的算法，证明其能产生最优解，并用伪代码总结算法；讨论超胞数量随网格密度的缩放关系。

Result: 通过对多种体系进行数值计算，验证该方法具有显著的计算优势，且优势随 q 点网格密度的增大而增强；证明了算法的最优性并给出超胞数量随网格密度的缩放行为。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持非对角超胞法优点的同时去除了按 q 点逐个构建超胞的冗余，能极大提升稠密 q 网格下声子计算的效率，为固体热学性质（如热容、热膨胀、热导率等）的高精度计算提供了更经济的途径。

Abstract: Phonon dynamics determine a vast number of properties in the solid state, such as the heat capacity, thermal expansion, and thermal conductivity. Phonons are obtained through the dynamical matrix, which, in the frozen phonon approach, is calculated as a finite difference derivative of the analytical Hellmann-Feynman force. This approach unfortunately typically suffers from requiring very large supercells, even for modest $\mathbf{q}$-grids. The non-diagonal supercell method remedies this issue by determining the smallest supercell commensurate with a given $\mathbf{q}$-point, drastically reducing the supercell size, but in turn requiring a new supercell for each $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid. This introduces redundancy, however, since a single non-diagonal supercell may be commensurate with several $\mathbf{q}$-points. In this work I present a method to determine a minimal set of supercells, such that every $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid is commensurate with at least one supercell. I present an algorithm to solve the problem and prove that it yields the optimal solution, before summarizing the algorithm using pseudocode. The scaling of the number of supercells with the density of the grid is discussed. Numerical results on a variety of systems show the significant computational advantage of the method, which increases with the density of the grid.

</details>


### [119] [Transverse Shifts Driven by Geometric Orbital Torque in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34665)
*Runze Li,Yugui Yao,Zhi-Ming Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种由费米面各向异性驱动的轨道力矩所导致的界面反射横移新机制，并在无自旋轨道耦合的交变磁体中预测了内禀的空间自旋分离效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的界面反射反常横移通常被归因于自旋轨道耦合或其赝自旋类比。作者希望突破这一范式，寻找一种不依赖于自旋轨道耦合的全新几何机制，以解释并预测横移现象。

Method: 在全内反射过程中，入射波包以倏逝波形式进入各向异性的透射介质，界面法线方向的旋转对称性破缺对其施加轨道力矩。累积的角冲量（等于轨道力矩与驻留时间的乘积）导致横向空间位移。将该机制应用于无自旋轨道耦合的交变磁体，发现轨道力矩对自旋具有奇宇称。

Result: 两个自旋通道被偏转到入射面的相反两侧，从而在交变磁体中产生内禀的空间自旋分离。该横移继承了交变磁自旋劈裂的角对称性，并在全反射起始点附近被强烈增强。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种新的横移机制，预言了交变磁体中新颖的自旋分离效应，并为直接探测交变磁序提供了手段。

Abstract: Anomalous transverse shifts in interface reflection are conventionally attributed to spin-orbit coupling or its pseudospin analogue. Here, we uncover a distinct geometric mechanism driven solely by an orbital torque arising from Fermi-surface anisotropy. During total reflection, an incident wave packet penetrates the anisotropic transmitted medium as an evanescent wave. There, the broken rotational symmetry about the interface normal exerts an orbital torque, and the accumulated angular impulse---the product of the torque and the dwell time---leads to a transverse spatial shift. Applying this mechanism to altermagnets without spin-orbit coupling, we show that the orbital torque is odd in spin. As a consequence, the two spin channels are deflected to opposite sides of the incident plane, giving an intrinsic spatial spin separation in altermagnets. The shift also inherits the angular symmetry of the altermagnetic spin splitting and is strongly enhanced near the onset of total reflection. Our work thus uncovers a new mechanism for transverse shifts, predicts a novel spin separation effect in altermagnets, and offers a direct probe of the altermagnetic order.

</details>


### [120] [Measuring a buried Bi$_2$Te$_3$ epitaxial film through a protective Te cap: an exact three-layer terahertz analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34847)
*Guilherme R. Russo,Laura O. Dias,Eduardo D. Stefanato,Nicolas M. Kawahala,Sukarno O. Ferreira,Leonarde N. Rodrigues,Felix G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对空气敏感的硫族化物薄膜（如 Bi₂Te₃），提出用太赫兹时域光谱（THz-TDS）免去除覆盖层即可定量表征薄膜电输运性质的方法，建立了覆盖层/薄膜/衬底三明治结构的精确传输矩阵模型，并给出了薄层极限下的闭式反演公式及忽略覆盖层所引入误差的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气敏感的硫族化物薄膜通常需要覆盖层保护，但传统电学测量必须先去除覆盖层或穿透其接触，从而破坏被保护表面。作者希望找到一种无需去除覆盖层、且能在定量上正确处理覆盖层影响的表征手段。

Method: 采用太赫兹时域光谱，并用含时间门控衬底的传输矩阵精确处理覆盖层/薄膜/衬底三层问题；证明相邻两薄层各自贡献独立法布里-珀罗因子的直观做法不成立；在薄层极限下推导出闭式反演公式，并给出忽略覆盖层引起的误差判据。实验上以分子束外延在 GaAs 上生长三种厚度的 Bi₂Te₃ 薄膜，每种分别制备有与无 25 nm Te 覆盖层，并设置 Te-on-GaAs 对照样品。

Result: Te-on-GaAs 对照显示覆盖层在 0.2–1.5 THz 内与裸衬底光学上不可区分，透射衬底幅度的 1.00(4)，因此忽略覆盖层对 90 nm 膜的误差被限制在 3.6%，但对 25 nm 膜同类估计达 48%。在这组配对样品中，有覆盖层的薄膜在电导幅值和线形上都随厚度单调有序，而无覆盖层的薄膜则不然。

Conclusion: 覆盖层对太赫兹表征并不构成障碍，且在本组样品中有覆盖层的薄膜响应更具可重复性，说明 THz-TDS 可作为空气敏感薄膜无损定量表征的有效手段。

Abstract: Air-sensitive chalcogenide films are routinely protected by a capping layer, which must then be removed, or contacted through, before electronic transport can be measured, destroying the surface the cap was meant to preserve. Terahertz time-domain spectroscopy requires neither, but using it quantitatively demands a transmission model in which cap, film and substrate are treated on an equal footing. We formulate the cap/film/substrate problem exactly using a transfer matrix with a time-gated substrate, and show that the intuitive construction in which each thin layer contributes an independent Fabry--Pérot factor fails for two adjacent thin films. In the thin-film limit we obtain a closed-form inversion together with a criterion for the error incurred by ignoring the cap. We apply the framework to Bi$_2$Te$_3$ films grown by molecular beam epitaxy on GaAs at three thicknesses, each prepared with and without a 25 nm Te cap. A Te-on-GaAs witness shows the cap to be optically indistinguishable from bare substrate, transmitting 1.00(4) of the substrate amplitude between 0.2 and 1.5 THz, which limits the error from neglecting it to 3.6 % for the 90 nm film, although the same estimate reaches 48 % at 25 nm. In this matched set, the capped films form an ordered series, monotonic in thickness in both conductance magnitude and lineshape, whereas the uncapped films do not. The capping layer therefore presents no obstacle to terahertz characterization, and in this set it coincides with a more reproducible film response.

</details>

### [121] [Exact Lyapunov spectrum and localization length of the multichannel disordered Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34455)
*Aurélien Grabsch*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究有限宽度导线中N个横向模的多通道薛定谔方程，在幺正对称类的白噪声势下，精确计算了任意能量和任意无序强度下的Lyapunov指数谱，发现高能区等间距性在任意能量下被破坏，并揭示局域化长度与态密度的直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往DMPK方法仅在高能区预测Lyapunov指数等间距分布，作者希望突破该限制，探究任意能量与任意无序强度下Lyapunov谱的真实结构，并揭示局域化长度与态密度的联系。

Method: 将问题化为修正Riccati矩阵的矩阵随机微分方程，对幺正对称类的多通道薛定谔方程进行精确分析，从而解析计算全部N个Lyapunov指数。

Result: 在任意能量E与无序强度T下精确得到Lyapunov指数集合{γ_n(E,T)}；高能区恢复等间距谱，但任意能量下等间距性被破坏，谱结构更丰富；同时发现局域化长度（最小Lyapunov指数之倒数）与态密度之间存在直接关系。

Conclusion: DMPK的等间距结论仅是高能近似，任意能量下Lyapunov谱更复杂；局域化长度与态密度之间存在普适联系，为多通道无序系统的波函数衰减行为提供了精确描述。

Abstract: We consider the multichannel Schrödinger equation, describing the evolution of $N$ transverse modes in a finite width wire, in the presence of a Gaussian white noise potential in the unitary symmetry class. For arbitrary energy $E$ and disorder strength $T$, we compute exactly the set of Lyapunov exponents $\{ γ_n(E,T) \}_{1 \leq n \leq N}$ which define the decay rates of the wave functions. In the large energy regime, we recover that the Lyapunov exponents are equally spaced, as predicted by the so-called DMPK approach. However we show that this property breaks down at arbitrary energy, giving rise to a richer Lyapunov spectrum. We also uncover a direct relation between the localization length, given by the inverse of the smallest Lyapunov exponent, and the density of states. Our derivation relies on the analysis of matrix stochastic differential equation for a modified Riccati matrix.

</details>


### [122] [From Spin Glass Theory to Optimization in High Dimensions and Modern Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34713)
*Riccardo Zecchina,Gianmarco Perrupato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Giorgio Parisi在自旋玻璃与副本对称破缺理论上的工作如何影响组合优化与人工神经网络，重点讨论复制法、腔法、survey propagation、解空间几何、计算复杂度与学习之间的联系，以及高局部熵稀有区域在算法可达性中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致敬Giorgio Parisi，梳理其自旋玻璃与副本对称破缺（RSB）理论对组合优化和人工神经网络领域的深远影响，揭示统计物理方法如何为理解计算复杂度、学习与推断提供统一框架。

Method: 通过匹配问题和旅行商问题引入复制法与腔法，随后讨论随机可满足性问题及survey propagation的发展，并将这些方法推广到神经网络，结合过度参数化模型与非对称递归网络等实例，分析解空间的几何结构、计算难度与算法可达性。

Result: 展示了复制/腔方法与survey propagation在组合优化中的有效性，阐明了高局部熵稀有区域对算法可达性的关键作用，并揭示过度参数化模型与非对称递归网络如何将约束优化、推断与自旋玻璃理论的概念相连接。

Conclusion: Parisi的自旋玻璃与RSB理论为组合优化和神经网络提供了深刻洞见，特别是解空间几何、计算硬度与学习之间的内在联系，以及高局部熵稀有区域的重要意义，表明统计物理方法在理解现代机器学习问题上仍具广阔前景。

Abstract: This chapter pays tribute to Giorgio Parisi by reviewing the influence of his work on spin glasses and replica symmetry breaking (RSB) on combinatorial optimization and artificial neural networks. We first introduce replica and cavity methods through the matching and traveling salesman problems, before discussing random satisfiability and the development of survey propagation. We then examine applications to neural networks, emphasizing the connections between the geometry of solution spaces, computational hardness, and learning. Particular attention is given to rare regions of high local entropy and their role in algorithmic accessibility. Examples involving overparameterized models and asymmetric recurrent networks illustrate how these ideas connect constrained optimization and inference with concepts developed in spin-glass theory.

</details>


### [123] [Magnons in solids with correlated atomic and nanoscale disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34926)
*Alex Moorhouse,Joseph Barker,Michele Simoncelli*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套理论计算框架，用于在原子尺度上定量预测缺陷与无序对磁性材料磁振子（magnon）谱函数的影响，并以含空位缺陷的亚铁磁体为例，结合机器学习势展示了空位空间分布对磁振子能带模糊化的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实磁性材料中的原子缺陷和无序会同时影响微观磁振子激发与宏观物理性质（如自旋刚度），但两者之间的定量关联长期以来难以建立，原因在于需要同时以量子精度模拟原子/纳米尺度的不均匀性与关联性，并准确计算其对磁振子谱函数的影响。

Method: 作者开发了三项核心技术：（1）COSINE（Coupled Optimization of SpIN Environment）算法，用于将含缺陷或无序固体的原子结构与磁构型高效弛豫到局域能量极小；（2）一种考虑局域成分、结构与磁矩不均匀性的交换相互作用高效获取方法；（3）面向 Bogoliubov 玻色子的 paraunitary unfolding（准幺正展开）方法，用于预测任意磁序（含倾斜、完全/部分/无磁矩补偿）材料中的磁振子谱函数。并将上述方法与先进的 spin-informed 机器学习势结合，针对代表性缺陷亚铁磁体进行演示。

Result: 该方法成功在代表性缺陷亚铁磁体中展示了通用性，并定量预测了空位缺陷空间分布的非均匀性如何控制有效磁振子能带结构的模糊化（blurring）程度。

Conclusion: 该理论计算框架弥补了量子精度模拟与真实磁序/缺陷体系磁振子谱预测之间的空白，为理解缺陷、无序与磁振子激发及宏观磁性之间的定量关系提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Atomic defects and disorder affect both the microscopic magnon excitations and the macroscopic physical properties of real-world magnetic materials. The rationalization of this relation has hitherto remained elusive, because of the intertwined challenges of simulating with quantum accuracy atomic- and nanoscale inhomogeneities and correlations, and their effect on the magnon spectral function determining observables such as spin stiffness. Here, we introduce a theoretical and computational framework to address these challenges. First, we develop the Coupled Optimization of SpIN Environment (COSINE) algorithm for efficiently relaxing the atomic structure and magnetic configuration of a solid with defects or disorder to a local energy minimum. Then, we discuss how to efficiently obtain exchange interactions accounting for local compositional, structural, and magnetic-moment inhomogeneities. Finally, we develop a paraunitary unfolding approach for Bogoliubov bosons, which allows us to predict the magnon spectral function in materials with any magnetic order, including systems with canting and full, partial, or no magnetic-moment compensation. We showcase our approach in a representative defective ferrimagnet for generality, and then combine it with state-of-the-art spin-informed machine-learning potentials to quantitatively predict how nonuniformities in the spatial distribution of vacancy defects control blurring of effective magnon bandstructures.

</details>
